# 📝 Changelog

> Complete version history for Antigravity Tools. Return to project home at [README.md](README.md).

*   **Version History**:
    *   **v5.0.4-beta.0 (2026-11-06)**:
        -   **[Native Android Architecture: Termux ARM64 Container-Free Deployment & Headless Runtime] (Fixes #3818, Thanks to @sandwitchers)**:
            -   **Android Termux Native Support**: Added native `aarch64-linux-android` (Bionic libc) backend target and one-click installation script `scripts/install-termux.sh`, completely freeing Android devices from container overhead (PRoot / chroot / Docker) for ultra-lightweight, energy-efficient local API gateway and Web UI execution. (Fixes #3818, Thanks to @sandwitchers)
            -   **Zero-Account Web Deadlock Resolution**: Fixed middleware deadlock in static asset and authentication filtering when zero accounts exist or the proxy service is inactive, ensuring seamless access to the Web console and account onboarding on cold boots. (Fixes #3818)
            -   **Cascading Static Asset Path Resolution**: Introduced `resolve_static_dist_path` algorithm supporting `ABV_DIST_PATH` env override and cascaded directory probing (`./dist`, `<exe_dir>/dist`, `$PREFIX/share/antigravity/dist`), eliminating CWD execution path dependencies. (Fixes #3818)
            -   **CI Workflow & Distribution Packaging**: Updated the Android release workflow to bundle frontend `dist` assets into `antigravity-server-android-arm64.tar.gz`, with the installer automatically deploying it to the standard share path. (Fixes #3818)
            -   **Server Command-Line Help Interception Fix**: Pre-empted `--help` / `-h` before generic CLI parsing, restoring the display of server flags (`-p`, `-h`, `--no-web`, `--pure-proxy`). (Fixes #3818)
            -   **Web UI Model Probe Endpoint Parity**: Registered the `POST /api/proxy/test-model` route on the headless admin server, resolving the 405 Method Not Allowed error when clicking the "Test" button on models in the Web UI. (Fixes #3818)

    *   **v5.0.3 (2026-11-06)**:
        -   **[Quota Attribution Governance: Root-Fixing 503 Misattribution & Quota False-Death; Refactoring Bucket Scheduling and Priority Competition] (Fixes #3838, #3841, #3821, PR #3845, Ref #3827, #3840)**:
            -   **Eradicate 503 Attribution Pollution (92.6% of all 5xx)**: Unified all internal gateway throttling predicates into the single source of truth `policy::is_internal_gateway_exhaustion`, covering the previously mis-detected `All accounts rate-limited` (the old list only matched `all accounts limited`), so local pool throttling is no longer reported as an upstream 503; converged the two other drifting implementations in `rate_limit.rs` and `token_manager.rs`. (Fixes #3838)
            -   **Dedicated 403 Ban Attribution**: Added the `account_forbidden` error code with 12-language copy for `TOS_VIOLATION` / `PERMISSION_DENIED`, so a banned account is no longer conflated with capacity exhaustion after the 403→503 mapping. (Fixes #3838)
            -   **Decouple Quota Exhaustion from the Adaptive Circuit Breaker**: Established the invariant that an upstream-declared quota exhaustion is an objective physical fact and must never be gated by the breaker switch. Fixed the defect where `if !config.enabled { return; }` short-circuited before the quota predicate, silently dropping `Individual quota reached` so the account was neither zeroed nor locked; added `is_quota_exhausted_or_explicit_limited` so `QuotaExhausted` accounts are intercepted even with the breaker disabled. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Account Exclusion in Retry Loops**: Each protocol retry loop now maintains `attempted_accounts` and passes it into `get_token_filtered`, eliminating the P2C behavior that kept re-selecting an account that had just returned 429 based on historical quota weights, and failing fast once the pool is exhausted. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Eliminate Zero-Quota High-Priority Pool Starvation (Priority Inversion Deadlock)**: Moved the target-model positive-quota filter ahead of priority convergence in `select_with_p2c`, establishing that “priority only competes among healthy accounts holding a valid positive quota for that model”; fixed the severe defect where a drained account boosted to `priority=1` still evicted every healthy full-quota account and returned “no available accounts”. (Ref #3840)
            -   **Authoritative Quota Diagnostics from Upstream**: Added L1 `quotaResetTimeStamp` priority locking so all four bucket classes (Argon standalone / Ultra shared / standard Gemini / 3P) adopt the official recovery instant when exhausted; the lock source now prefers hard quotas, the false-full blind spot caused by an omitted `remainingFraction` is fixed, and the all-pool-zero-quota pass-through hole is closed. (Fixes #3841)
            -   **Frontend Circuit Breaker Default Corrected**: `circuit_breaker` now initializes with `enabled: true`, preventing an accidental breaker shutdown when saving the settings page. (PR #3845)
        -   **[Pedal Mode Rebuilt as Top Scheduling Priority] (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:
            -   **From Hard Disabling to Priority Competition**: Activating pedal mode no longer suspends other accounts via `proxy_disabled = true`; expiring accounts are instead promoted to priority `1` (default 50). This eliminates three pathologies at once: Prompt Cache invalidation from forced failover, 429s from squeezing all concurrency onto a single account, and cross-model collateral damage from account-level disabling. Existing sessions stay bound to their account to preserve cache, new sessions consume the expiring quota first, and concurrent traffic spills over automatically. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Smooth Automatic Recovery**: Once the pedal account's weekly quota is fully drained (0%) or its reset time arrives, original priorities are restored precisely from `originalPriorities`, with tolerant cleanup retained for stale suspended state left by older versions. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
        -   **[Timeout Contract and Stream Lifecycle Unification: Hard Truncation Removed, Four Protocols Share One Pipeline] (Fixes #3816, Fixes #3821, Ref #3763, PR #3817)**:
            -   **Hardcoded Truncation Abolished**: Retired the 135s/160s hard truncation (below the measured medium p90 of 130.9s, which reversed legitimately slow requests into 504s and triggered retry storms); the first-byte baseline is restored to 180s, multimodal ceiling to 270s, and the 270s global request budget is pushed down into the pipeline, short-circuiting immediately when remaining budget is insufficient. (Fixes #3821)
            -   **Codex / Legacy Converged onto the Shared Lifecycle**: `create_codex_sse_stream` and `create_legacy_sse_stream` moved from hand-rolled `select!` drivers to `ProtocolStreamHandler` adapters under `run_stream_lifecycle`, unifying 3s heartbeats, initial-TTFT grace, thinking/content phase promotion, steady-state sliding timeouts, and abnormal finalization; Gemini gained `is_thinking_active` so the thinking phase is no longer misjudged as a transition cliff. (Ref #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Transition-Cliff Adaptive Promotion**: Fixed the failure where a deep-thinking model emits thought blocks and then goes silent while upstream buffers a monolithic `functionCall`, causing the 120s steady-state timeout to truncate the stream into a false HTTP 200 with no output; the silent window now promotes to the `Transition` phase with a 180s grace window and `: ping` keep-alives. (Fixes #3816, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)
            -   **Sticky Unbinding Across All Terminal States**: `TerminalDisposition` now folds `FirstByte` / `Upload` / `GlobalBudget` into `abandon_sticky_account`, eliminating the deadlock where clients waited out multiple three-minute attempts on the same slow account. (Fixes #3821)
            -   **Upload and Replay Guards**: Upload stall timeouts are decoupled from the adaptive upload budget and floored at the connect timeout; the cross-account replay threshold for large bodies is widened to 4MB with a cap of 3 attempts to suppress upstream amplification. (Fixes #3821)
        -   **[Model Routing and Gates: Custom Mapping Takes Top Precedence, Legacy Models Hard-Retired] (Fixes #2065, Ref #3764, #3769, #3815, PR #3787)**:
            -   **Four-Stage Inbound Model Gate**: Missing model → 404 `model not exist`; custom mappings first (exact / case-insensitive / normalized / wildcard); the resolved target must exist in “downstream exposure ∪ agent tool list ∪ official catalog ∪ mapping values”; all-pool zero quota → 502 `no quota accounts`; fail-open on cold start without metadata, and gateway self-answered 404/502 go through the single-source `is_gateway_self_answer` exemption so accounts are never locked or unbound. (Ref #3764, #3769)
            -   **Custom Mappings Elevated to the Very Front**: Ahead of official hot-update deprecation redirects, fixing the post-hoc `resolve_bare_tiered_model_route` rewrite into `tiered`/`medium`; the default tier selector flipped to prefer High, with in-place promotion to High on upstream 503 `MODEL_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED`.
            -   **Legacy Model Retirement with Map-Before-Judge Ordering**: Hard gates for Claude 3.x / < 4.6 and Gemini 2.x (image-generation families exempted independently), with the “map first, then judge” order locked so legacy names remain valid inbound aliases. (Ref #3815)
            -   **Pool-Adaptive Dark Routing**: Removed the static `CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI` hardcoded map and added `auto:gemini-flash-low` / `auto:claude-opus` / `auto:claude-opus-4-7` adaptive targets that pick and downgrade among Opus 5.5 / 4.6 / 4.7 by live positive quota; `custom_mapping` is decoupled from `custom_mapping_expose` with a `builtin_mapping_version` seamless migration. (Fixes #2065, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Upstream Physical Model Convergence**: Removed the `modelVersion` override of `upstream_model` (internal build names polluted the already-arbitrated routing result); `X-Upstream-Model` is now the single authoritative source across protocols. (Ref #3658, #3654)
            -   **Official Thinking Migration Alignment**: `thinkingLevel` first with `thinkingBudget` fallback; outbound fingerprint calibrated to 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195; model lists and one-click presets expose only models an account can actually use. (Ref #3751)
        -   **[Multimodal Self-Healing and Freshness Sliding Window: In-Place Bad-Image Repair, Responses Protocol Parity] (Fixes #3758, #3824, #3756, PR #3759, PR #3830)**:
            -   **In-Place Bad-Image 400 Self-Healing**: On `400 Unable to process input image` the pipeline no longer locks the account or unbinds stickiness (preserving Prefix Cache), replacing the corrupted image with a structured placeholder and retrying in place; single-image cases record a SHA-256 fingerprint into `ImageBlacklist` (FIFO, cap 2000) for persistent immunity, while multi-image cases are replaced for the current turn only to avoid false positives. (Fixes #3758, PR #3759, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Multimodal Freshness Sliding Window Enabled by Default**: `enable_sliding_window = true` keeping the latest 5 images by default; with the window enabled the gateway entry hard-limit on accumulated images is bypassed so the pipeline can prune, preventing long Codex conversations from being killed. (Fixes #3824)
            -   **Responses Protocol Parity**: Simple-log mode now recursively redacts Base64 in `input_image`, inline Data URLs, and `function_call_output`; the token estimator gained a Responses branch with vision-token accounting. (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)
            -   **Image-Generation Watchdog Widened with Empty-Image Diagnostics**: Primary endpoint 90s, fallback 45s, total budget 135s; a protocol-agnostic empty-image diagnostic extractor means an upstream 200 without an image is no longer treated as a false success. (Fixes #3756)
            -   **Cowork Auto-Compact Alignment**: Removed the blanket 1M-model exemption and the mid-tool-call block, triggering compression precisely at `effective_threshold`, backed by a 10-case dual-track control matrix unit test. (Fixes #3821, PR #3835, PR #3836, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Prompt Sanitization Track Separation and Inbound Security Hardening] (Fixes #3764, #3781, Ref #425, #146, PR #3746)**:
            -   **Sanitization Tracks Converged to System / Content / Anchored**: The current turn only sanitizes inside `<system*>` / `<identity*>` tags while user text outside them is preserved verbatim, so single-turn translation, proofreading, and code-audit requests are no longer rewritten into an official identity; the injection-zone boundary is determined by “the last text part of the last user message”. (Fixes #3764)
            -   **Dual Identity-Regex Protection and Multi-Pass Idempotence**: Bridge segments no longer span lines, with interrogative and cross-clause exemptions added, protecting `compute_prefix_hash` prefix stability and Prompt Caching. (Fixes #3764)
            -   **Adaptive CTF / Security-Policy Stripping**: Injected CTF and defensive-disclaimer text is stripped on both the System and full-text tracks, code blocks are deeply isolated, and empty parts are physically removed (an empty `systemInstruction` is unregistered). (Fixes #3781)
            -   **Inbound Gzip Decompression**: Mounted `RequestDecompressionLayer` after `auth` (zip-bomb defense) and before `monitor` (so auditing sees the real decoded text). (Ref #425, #146, PR #3746, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Account System and Enterprise Access: GCP Vertex AI, IDE 2.6.0 Switching, Multi-Link Consistency] (Fixes #3823, #3829, #3806, #3802, #3791, #3754)**:
            -   **GCP / Vertex AI Enterprise Accounts**: Aligned with the official enterprise client credential system, enabling standalone `antigravity_gcp` registration and dynamic authentication; the account detail dialog gained Project ID viewing/editing and one-click auto-fetch (`loadCodeAssist` sniffing `cloudaicompanionProject`, timeout 3s→12s, wired into the global proxy pool), and the misclassification of ordinary accounts as `ENTERPRISE` is fixed. (Fixes #3823)
            -   **Antigravity IDE 2.6.0+ Account Switching**: Triple-write across SQLite `state.vscdb`, the system Keyring, and file fallback, with `expires_in` / `id_token_email` field alignment; process identity collapsed into the single `classify_process` entry point that trusts only executable names/paths, preventing `taskkill /F /T` from killing VS Code; orphan `agy` language servers are swept per target. (Fixes #3762, #3770, #3778, #3829, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9, @fuyangtian2003-beep)
            -   **Classic Edition Switching Completed**: The local fallback credential `jetski-standalone-oauth-token` is now written uniformly, with an atomic backup plus dual-write of `state.vscdb`. (Fixes #3829)
            -   **Account Deletion Consistency**: The dispatch pool treats `accounts.json` as the sole admission list, logging orphan credential files instead of deleting them; the delete flow moves failure points to the front (commit the index before removing credential files). (Fixes #3806, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)
            -   **Web / Docker Links**: OAuth fixes (no duplicate Prepare, forced loopback fallback for the built-in client, dirty-state cleanup on exchange failure, resilient manual-input parsing); an opt-in “remember login credential” toggle; the update-settings endpoint now merges partial patches, fixing 400 responses and channel resets. (Fixes #3802, #3791, #3754, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dandelion-promise520)
            -   **DeepSeek Harness One-Click Sync**: Covers macOS / Windows / Linux, writing `cordis.patch.yml` and `.credentials.yaml`, with `antigravity-tools sync` added to the CLI; lookahead regex was replaced by a plain-text chunking state machine, eradicating Regex panics. (PR #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound)
        -   **[Platform Stability: Linux Remote-Desktop Keep-Alive, macOS Mini HUD, UI Zoom] (Fixes #3752, #3757, #3744, Ref #3748, PR #3766)**:
            -   **Linux X11/xrdp Keep-Alive**: Hooked `XSetIOErrorHandler` and rebuilt `_exit` into thread-level isolation so a remote-desktop disconnect no longer kills the 8045 proxy; GTK tray menus are dispatched to the main thread; xrdp / VNC sessions automatically enable WebKit software rendering. (Fixes #3752, #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **macOS Mini HUD**: `switch_to_main_window` now returns to the native main thread and Mini activation uses the `Accessory` policy, removing foreground cascade jumps and cross-thread deadlocks; the bottom CSS shadow was replaced with native rounded corners. (Fixes #3744, Ref #3748)
            -   **UI and Font Zoom**: Native `webview.setZoom()` on desktop with a CSS zoom fallback for the web, `Cmd/Ctrl + = / - / 0` shortcuts, 85%~150% presets, and a double guard that isolates Mini from zoom changes. (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)
            -   **Tray and Interaction**: New `tray_click_action` preference, 5px ultra-thin scrollbars that fade in on hover, and a temporary APIKEY.FUN Double 11 badge (with a `bump-version` expiry reminder). (Ref #3748)
        -   **[Performance and Observability: Tool Signature Cache Rebuild, Token Dual-Perspective Stats, QoS Deadlock Fix] (PR #3765, PR #3774, Fixes #3810, #3749, #3783)**:
            -   **Tool Signature Cache Rebuild**: L1 moved from `Mutex<HashMap>` to sharded `DashMap` with capacity raised 500→10000; a new `ag-tool-sig-writer` background thread (bounded queue, up to 512 rows per transaction) plus `PERSIST_SEQ` two-level watermarks prevents dirty-signature resurrection; the read path batches `IN` queries with a 4-connection read-only pool; a new `CacheProfile` adapts to CPU, memory, and deployment shape. (PR #3765, PR #3774, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Token Dual-Perspective Stats + TTFT**: `token_usage` gained `requested_model` / `ttft_ms`, separating the “actual routed model” from the “requested model” with automatic historical migration; first-token latency is integrated across the overview, deep-insight, and classic dashboards, and the control bar was redesigned for minimalism. (Fixes #3810)
            -   **QoS Fixes and Switch**: Fixed the DashMap same-thread read/write self-deadlock in the expiry sweeper (two-phase collect-then-swap); added `qos_auto_jail` (default off) which clears the whole pool's jails and skips strike evaluation when disabled. (Fixes #3749, #3783, Thanks to @TakimotoUki)
            -   **True TTFT Closure**: `StreamTtftCapture` backfills the pure first-chunk latency of the effective endpoint after the stream completes, with frontend guards rejecting `<= 0`; Gemini stream interruptions now relay the real `finishMessage` and `gatewayError`. (Fixes #3813)
        -   **[Scheduling Stickiness and 3P Quota Governance] (Fixes #3789, #3809, #3795, #3750, #3797)**:
            -   **Multi-Agent Root Session Normalization**: `extract_root_session_id` trims derived suffixes such as `:step:N` / `:agent:...` / `--step-N` so the affinity key collapses onto the root session; added `AffinityScope::ApiKey` for credential-level strong stickiness (`ANTIGRAVITY_SCHEDULING_AFFINITY_SCOPE`). (Fixes #3789)
            -   **3P Phantom Quota Governance**: The generic 3P quota group is stripped when an account has no Claude/GPT entitlement, rendering `-` placeholders instead of a false 100%; the default quota refresh interval moved from 15 to 10 minutes. (Fixes #3809)
            -   **Argon / Ultra Convergence**: Unified `hasAccountArgonCapability` and `checkArgonEligibility` criteria; Ultra legally inherits the shared Gemini quota pool, fixing scheduling 503s and hidden badges. (Fixes #3750)
            -   **Hub Fingerprint and Gate Diagnostics**: The official Hub fingerprint was upgraded to 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195 with asynchronous Headless detection; 12-language gateway error diagnostics and a settings-save fix were added. (Fixes #3797, Ref #3750)
        -   **[Release Notes] (Ref #3845)**: This is the stable promotion of the 5.0.3 pre-release line (`beta.0` ~ `beta.5`) and is functionally identical to the latest beta; `v5.0.3-beta.4` never produced a Release because of a transient GitHub API 500 in the publish step, and its fix shipped with the following versions. Thanks to every contributor in this cycle: @cubelikeplayDaniel, @mclinyeshi, @modded-soldier-9, @zepeng-jin, @fuyangtian2003-beep, @wakeupnotfound, @wthsama, @nicolastinkl, @hazerwin, @ddmixi, @dandelion-promise520, @dnmdngcsb, @EricZhou05, @Ran-Xing, @TakimotoUki, @W2514, @lbjlaq.

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.5 (2026-11-06)**:
        -   **[Decouple Physical Quota Exhaustion from Adaptive Circuit Breaker; Exclude Attempted Accounts in Retry Loops] (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Physical fact takes precedence over adaptive strategy**: Decoupled “upstream-declared quota exhaustion” from the “adaptive circuit breaker backoff switch”. Previously `if !config.enabled { return; }` ran before the quota predicate, so when Google returned an explicit `Individual quota reached`, the signal was dropped — the account was neither marked 0% nor locked. Now the invariant is established: an upstream-declared quota exhaustion is an objective physical fact and must never be gated by the breaker switch. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Attempted-account exclusion in retry loops**: Fixed repeated hammering of the same drained account. Previously `get_token` received an empty `excluded_accounts`, so P2C could re-select the just-429'd account due to its historical quota weight. Each protocol retry loop now maintains `attempted_accounts` and passes it to `get_token_filtered`, failing fast after a full pool sweep. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **No longer bypassing realtime locks when the breaker is off**: Added `is_quota_exhausted_or_explicit_limited`, so accounts in `QuotaExhausted` state are still correctly intercepted even with the breaker disabled.
            -   **Frontend breaker default corrected**: `circuit_breaker` now initializes with `enabled: true`, preventing users from accidentally disabling the breaker by saving settings. (PR #3845, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Fix beta.4 Release Pipeline Failure] (Ref #3845)**:
            -   **Background**: The beta.4 Release pipeline hit a transient GitHub API HTTP 500 at the `publish-release` step (all 10 build jobs succeeded), so the Release object was never created and `updater.json` was missing — users could not see that version.
            -   **Resolution**: Removed the dangling `v5.0.3-beta.4` tag and shipped beta.5 directly, delivering the PR #3845 fixes together with everything since beta.3.

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.4 (2026-11-05)**:
        -   **[Re-release: Fix Auto-Update Failure in beta.3] (Ref #3840)**:
            -   **Background**: After beta.3 shipped, a `cargo fmt` formatting fix required re-pointing the tag, which caused the Release workflow to run a second time for the same tag and overwrite the artifacts, changing their signatures. Clients already on beta.3 could not update in place due to signature mismatch and fell back to opening the download page.
            -   **Fix**: Ship beta.4 with a higher version number so existing users take the normal upgrade path and in-place auto-update works again. Functionally beta.4 is identical to beta.3 and contains only rustfmt formatting corrections.
            -   **Safeguard**: This release only tags after CI (including `cargo fmt --check`) is fully green, preventing repeated overwrites.

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.3 (2026-11-05)**:
        -   **[Eliminate 503 Misattribution and Quota False-Deadlock: Rebuild Bucket Scheduling, Custom Mapping Priority and Timeout Contract] (Fixes #3838, #3841, Ref #3821, #3827, #3840)**:
            -   **Root-cause fix for 503 misattribution (92.6% of all 5xx)**: Unified the internal rate-limit predicate into `policy::is_internal_gateway_exhaustion` as the single source of truth, and filled in the previously missed `All accounts rate-limited` (the old list only matched `all accounts limited`, causing local pool exhaustion to be misreported as upstream 503); also converged two other independently drifting implementations in `rate_limit.rs` and `token_manager.rs`. (Fixes #3838)
            -   **Dedicated 403 ban attribution**: Added `account_forbidden` error code plus 12-language copy for `TOS_VIOLATION` / `PERMISSION_DENIED`, so a banned account is no longer conflated with capacity exhaustion after 403→503 remapping. (Fixes #3838)
            -   **Quota diagnosis upgraded to trust upstream authoritative signals**: Added L1 locking that prefers the upstream `quotaResetTimeStamp`. All four quota bucket classes (Argon dedicated / Ultra shared / standard Gemini / 3P) carry it when exhausted, so trusting the official recovery instant is more reliable than local inference and immune to upstream copy changes; `is_quota_depleted_signal` is now case-insensitive, keeps `check quota` as the 1.5% bucket trigger, and explicitly excludes transient TPM throttling (preventing pool-wide cascading lockouts, Fixes #3506). (Fixes #3841)
            -   **Lock source ordered by hard-quota priority**: Bucket predicate relaxed from “weekly only” to “any bucket in the matched group ≤ 1.5%”. A drained weekly locks until its own reset; only when weekly is healthy and solely 5h is drained do we lock the short 5h cycle — avoiding both re-probing every time 5h recovers while weekly is still zero, and locking a recoverable-in-minutes account for a whole day.
            -   **Fixed quota false-full blind spots**: Handles upstream omitting `remainingFraction` for exhausted models (previously `unwrap_or(100)` read it as full), and unified multi-key tolerant quota matching across preferred accounts, sticky sessions and the 60s reuse window.
            -   **Quota scheduling closed loop**: Blocked the whole-pool 0-quota bare-run escape; fixed the 3P path of `accounts_with_positive_quota` missing an `available_models` fallback, which let a single drained 3P model trip the 502 `no quota accounts` gate for the entire pool; quota-exhausted 429 responses are now passed through verbatim without gateway rewriting.
            -   **Eliminated priority-inversion starvation**: Moved quota filtering ahead of priority convergence in `select_with_p2c`, establishing the invariant that “priority only competes among healthy accounts holding positive quota for the target model”. Fixes the severe defect where the pedal mode (or a manually boosted account) at `priority=1` with 0% quota and zero in-flight would evict all other full-quota accounts and return “no available accounts”. (Ref #3840)
            -   **Model routing: user custom mapping takes absolute precedence**: Custom mappings moved to the very front, ahead of official hot-update deprecation redirects; fixed the post-hoc `resolve_bare_tiered_model_route` rewrite that silently downgraded matched targets to `tiered`/`medium`; default tier selector flipped to prefer High (upstream medium/tiered are measured to be capacity-starved); added in-place promotion to High when upstream returns 503 `MODEL_CAPACITY_EXHAUSTED`.
            -   **Timeout contract: hard truncation removed, frontend/backend aligned**: Abolished the 135s/160s hard-coded cap (below the measured medium p90 of 130.9s, which turned legitimately slow requests into 504 and triggered retry storms); first-byte baseline restored to 180s with multimodal ceiling widened to 270s; wired the frontend large-body replay threshold so the backend 4MB hardcode no longer drifts from the frontend 8MB setting.
        -   **[Pedal Quota Mode Rebuilt as Highest Scheduling Priority] (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:
            -   **Brute-force suspension replaced by priority competition**: Activating pedal mode no longer suspends other accounts via `proxy_disabled = true`; instead it boosts imminent accounts to scheduling priority `1` (default 50). Eliminates three pathologies at once: Prompt Caching invalidation from forced failover of ongoing sessions, 429s from crushing all concurrency onto a single account, and cross-model collateral damage from entity-level disabling. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Concurrency resilience**: Existing sessions stay bound to their accounts to preserve cache; new sessions fully prioritize burning the imminent account, with overflow spilling to other idle accounts.
            -   **Smooth automatic recovery**: Once target accounts fully drain (0%) or reach their reset time, original priorities are restored precisely from `originalPriorities`; legacy suspended-state cleanup is retained for backward compatibility. (PR #3840, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.2 (2026-11-04)**:
        -   **[Fix Primary Daily Endpoint Jitter Leading to Sandbox Fallback Endpoint Poisoning] (Fixes #3827, PR #3833, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **Sandbox Endpoint Isolation & Anti-Leapfrog Priority Partitioning**: Introduced sandbox endpoint detection and partitioned sorting so `daily-cloudcode-pa.sandbox.googleapis.com` never leapfrogs primary Daily endpoints due to health stats; eliminates accidental account poisoning and false 504 Gateway Timeouts triggered when non-US proxies receive 400 (User location is not supported) from sandbox endpoints. (Fixes #3827, PR #3833, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Sandbox 400 Degradation & Extended Cooldown**: Reclassified sandbox 400 errors as eligible for next-endpoint rotation with an enforced minimum 300-second cooldown penalty, preventing repetitive fallback cascades.
        -   **[Fix Classic Client Credential Sync Missing Fallback Files and State Dual-Write] (Fixes #3829, PR #3834, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **Unified Credential Dual-Write across Classic and IDE Modes**: Fixed an issue where switching accounts in Classic mode (`target_ide == "classic"`) prompted for re-authentication on desktop restart; unified the fallback credential writer (`jetski-standalone-oauth-token`) to apply seamlessly across both Classic and IDE runtimes. (Fixes #3829, PR #3834, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Safe SQLite Atomic Backup and State Ingestion**: When `state.vscdb` is present in the Classic data directory, atomically backs up the database and dual-writes the authentication token and `serviceMachineId` to maintain strict state parity.
        -   **[Enterprise & GCP Vertex AI Project ID Management and Auto-Discovery] (Fixes #3823)**:
            -   **Visual GCP Project ID Configuration & Persistence**: Added a dedicated Project ID setting within the Account Details modal, allowing users to inspect, manually set, or modify the linked Google Cloud Project ID for Enterprise/GCP accounts, synchronously persisted to local credentials and gateway memory.
            -   **One-Click Auto-Fetch Project ID**: Implemented the `auto_fetch_account_project_id` command with an "Auto Fetch" button in the GUI, automatically querying `loadCodeAssist` to discover and associate the account's upstream `cloudaicompanionProject`.
            -   **Proxy Pool Integration & Timeout Hardening**: Relaxed the query timeout from an aggressive 3 seconds to 12 seconds and routed requests through the global `ProxyPool`, ensuring high reliability across proxies and international latency variations.
            -   **Precise Enterprise Tier Classification**: Fixed an issue where the built-in standard client key (`antigravity_enterprise`) or legacy `is_gcp_tos` flags mistakenly caused personal accounts to be classified as `ENTERPRISE`, strictly reserving the tier for true `antigravity_gcp` clients and authoritative subscription levels.
            -   **Comprehensive 12-Language i18n**: Added complete translations for the Project ID controls, descriptions, placeholders, and status toasts across 12 languages (AR, EN, ES, JA, KO, MY, PT, RU, TR, VI, ZH, ZH-TW).
        -   **[Fix Responses Protocol Multimodal Sliding Window Ingress Guard, Payload Audit Redaction, and Token Estimation] (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)**:
            -   **Bypass Cumulative Image Byte Interception when Sliding Window is Active**: Fixed an issue where enabling the multimodal sliding freshness window (`enable_sliding_window = true`) still triggered an ingress HTTP 400 rejection if historical, unpruned images accumulated beyond 32MB; cumulative payload limits are now cleanly deferred to pipeline sliding window pruning while retaining single-image limits (>20MB), preventing multimodal Agent sessions (e.g. Codex) from being falsely blocked at entry. (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)
            -   **Comprehensive Responses Base64 Image Audit Redaction**: In simple audit mode, recursively sanitized inline Data URLs, `input_image` blocks, and `function_call_output` images into lightweight placeholders (`[base64 image: xxx bytes]`), preventing SQLite database bloating and UI sluggishness during multiround image inspection tasks. (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)
            -   **Responses Multimodal Visual Token Estimation**: Added native Responses payload parsing in `PipelineTokenEstimator`, correctly converting multimodal images into visual tokens and preventing token underestimation from skewing pipeline timeout budgets. (PR #3830, Thanks to @mclinyeshi)
        -   **[Fix Long-Context Conversations Mistakenly Triggering Cowork Auto-Compaction & Experiment Matrix] (Fixes #3821, Ref #3826, PR #3835, PR #3836, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Align Configured Auto-Compaction Guard with Tool Turn Ingress**: Removed the one-size-fits-all 1M exemption and premature tool interruptions, ensuring authentic Cowork sessions containing tool results trigger self-healing auto-compaction strictly per GUI-configured `effective_threshold`; resolved an issue where upload budgets were clipped by TTFT offsets for large payloads. (Fixes #3821, Ref #3826, PR #3835, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Full-Chain 10-Case Dual-Track Control Experiment Matrix**: Added `test_cowork_10_control_experiment_matrix` unit tests thoroughly asserting 5 oversize clipping conditions and 5 safe passthrough conditions, ensuring gateway guards stay 100% consistent with configuration thresholds. (Fixes #3821, Ref #3826, PR #3836, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.1 (2026-11-04)**:
        -   **[Official GCP Vertex AI Enterprise Account Support & Adaptive Credential Isolation] (Fixes #3823)**:
            -   **Aligned with Official GCP Enterprise OAuth Credentials**: Reverse-engineered and matched the official Antigravity application architecture for GCP / Vertex AI enterprise authorization, integrating the official GCP enterprise client (`884354919052-...`) under `antigravity_gcp`. Completely resolves the issue where enterprise accounts were misidentified as Free Tier or lost access to Vertex AI project quotas. (Fixes #3823)
            -   **Strict Backward Compatibility for Legacy Accounts**: Credentials strictly differentiate via `token.oauth_client_key` and `is_gcp_tos`; legacy accounts with custom domain emails (e.g. `@imsot.org`) previously authorized via the personal client retain 100% personal-mode refresh compatibility, preventing Google `400 invalid_grant` errors caused by naive domain-based client switching.
            -   **Top-Level Account Type Selector Across All Import Modes with Full i18n**: Redesigned the Add Account dialog by hoisting the "Personal Account" vs. "Enterprise / GCP (Vertex AI)" type switcher to the top-level common area across OAuth, Refresh Token, and DB import modes; added complete translations across 12 languages including JA, ZH-TW, KO, ES, PT, RU, TR, VI, AR, and MY. (Fixes #3823)
            -   **IDE Database Injection & Adaptive Refresh Integration**: Accurately toggles Protobuf Field 6 (`is_gcp_tos`) and maps the Vertex AI project when injecting credentials into the Antigravity IDE SQLite store (`state.vscdb`); ensures token refresh strictly honors the originating client descriptor.
        -   **[Fix Quiet Autostart Still Showing Window and Sync Autostart Config] (Fixes #3820, Thanks to @W2514)**:
            -   **Defensive Startup Argument Substring Matching**: Resolved an issue where quoted or concatenated startup parameters from certain Windows Run registry keys, shortcuts, or Task Scheduler instances failed strict equality comparisons (`== "--minimized"`), erroneously triggering an interactive window presentation on login; reinforced the check with substring pattern matching (`lower.contains("--minimized") || lower.contains("--silent") || lower.contains("--hidden")`), ensuring quiet login launch consistently stays in the system tray. (Fixes #3820, Thanks to @W2514)
            -   **Atomic Autostart State Persistence**: Synchronously updated and persisted `config.auto_launch` to the app configuration file whenever toggling autostart via `toggle_auto_launch`, eliminating state drift between UI controls and OS registry entries. (Fixes #3820)
        -   **[Default Multimodal Sliding Window & Automatic 403 Validation Circuit Breaking] (Fixes #3824)**:
            -   **Aligned with Official Antigravity Multimodal Pruning**: Reverse-engineered and matched the official Antigravity app's image cleanup behavior by enabling the multimodal sliding freshness window by default (`enable_sliding_window = true`) and keeping the latest 5 images (`max_fresh_images = 5`). Prevents multiround conversations from accumulating dozens of images, inflating request payloads to multi-megabytes, and triggering upload congestion or 504 Gateway Timeouts. (Fixes #3824)
            -   **Relaxed Adaptive Upload Bandwidth Floor**: Lowered the minimum upload speed baseline from 2.0 Mbps to 0.8 Mbps to offer higher tolerance for slow proxies and volatile cross-border networks during large payload uploads.
            -   **Full-Chain Circuit Breaking & Session Unbinding for 403 Challenges**: Fixed an issue where encountering Google's `VALIDATION_REQUIRED` challenge failed to unbind sticky sessions or filter snapshot pools, causing repeated client retries to deadlock on the same failing account. Sticky sessions are now immediately severed (`session_accounts.remove`), and pool selection automatically fails over to healthy accounts.
            -   **Automatic Proxy Disabling on 403**: Synchronously marks the account as `proxy_disabled = true` upon validation failure, immediately reflecting the disabled state on the GUI and cleanly removing it from proxy rotation.
        -   **[Client-Safe Gateway Timeout Clamping and Session Affinity Release on Terminal Failures] (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Client-Safe Timeout Hard Ceiling**: Introduced `MAX_CLIENT_SAFE_TOTAL_BUDGET_SECS = 160s` across the entire upstream call pipeline. Resolved an issue where long requests combining multimodal media offsets and multi-endpoint fallbacks ballooned to 190s~256s, exceeding client-side 180s watchdogs (such as JeikCode) and severing the connection into orphan canceled requests; guarantees a ≥20s safe window so the gateway always delivers a structured 504 error before the client disconnects. (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **First-Byte Budget Baseline and Media Cap Tightening**: Clamped `MAX_FIRST_BYTE_BUDGET_SECS` to 135s and the media-augmented ceiling `MAX_FIRST_BYTE_CEILING_SECS` to 145s; aligned the 200k+ large-context adaptive TTFT floor to 135s. (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Primary Endpoint First-Byte Grace**: Corrected `reserve_for_fallback` deduction so only secondary fallback endpoints (`idx > 0`) have 1/3 of the budget withheld; the primary endpoint (`idx == 0`) now receives full first-byte generation time, fulfilling the original intent for large-context and deep-reasoning models. (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Session Affinity Release on Terminal Failures**: Extended `pipeline::terminal::TerminalDisposition` to include `FirstByte`, `Upload`, and `GlobalBudget` in `abandon_sticky_account`; upstream queuing timeouts or upload stall interruptions immediately unbind session affinity so subsequent client retries automatically balance to healthy accounts across the pool, eliminating multi-round 3-minute lockups on a single stalled account. (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Phased Timeout Diagnosis and Stream Silence Clamping**: Corrected timeout classification in `send_with_phased_budget` so post-upload timeouts accurately report `stage=await_first_byte`; clamped `transition_secs` and `streaming_sliding_secs` in `StreamLifecycleConfig` to the global request remaining budget. (Fixes #3821, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Regression Testing**: Updated terminal affinity-release assertions and client timeout envelope tests; all 6 `terminal::tests` and 17 `client::tests` pass cleanly. (Fixes #3821)

    *   **v5.0.3-beta.0 (2026-11-03)**:
        -   **[Unified Streaming Lifecycle Across Protocols: Codex/Legacy Adopt the Pipeline, Gemini Gains Thinking-Phase Awareness] (Ref #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Codex / Legacy Converged onto the Shared Lifecycle**: Replaced the hand-rolled `select!` drivers in `create_codex_sse_stream` and `create_legacy_sse_stream` with `ProtocolStreamHandler` adapters driven by `run_stream_lifecycle`, centralizing 3s heartbeats, initial-TTFT grace, thinking/streaming phase promotion, steady-state sliding timeouts, and abnormal finalization. Removed the duplicated phase state machines and timeout branches while preserving Responses event streams, `text_completion` frames, tool-call caching, grounding citations, and session-persistence acknowledgements. (Ref #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Generic Termination Hooks in the Pipeline**: Added optional `emit_error_finalize`, `finalize_without_content`, and `finalize_barrier` hooks so Codex's `error + response.failed`, Legacy's error frame + single `[DONE]`, thinking-only EOF `response.incomplete`, and "emit `response.completed` only after session persistence succeeds" contracts remain unchanged after the migration. (Ref #3816, PR #3817)
            -   **Gemini Thinking-Phase Awareness**: Added `is_thinking_active` tracking to `GeminiStreamHandler` so thinking blocks, visible text, tool calls, and `finishReason` correctly drive `InitialTtft → Thinking → Streaming/Transition`, preventing thinking periods from being misclassified as a transition cliff. (Ref #3816, PR #3817)
            -   **Regression Coverage**: Added Legacy normal/error single-`[DONE]` tests and a Gemini thinking-phase transition test; all 22 OpenAI streaming, 6 Gemini streaming, and 10 lifecycle tests pass. (Ref #3816, PR #3817)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.9 (2026-11-03)**:
        -   **[Codex / Responses Stream Transition-Cliff Adaptive Promotion and First-Byte Budget Expansion] (Fixes #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @mclinyeshi, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Transition Cliff Adaptive Promotion and Heartbeat Keep-Alive**: Resolved a critical issue in Codex Desktop calling deep reasoning models like `gemini-4-argon-high`, where upstream pauses streaming while buffering monolithic `functionCall` payloads (such as large scripts or diagrams) after thinking output, causing the gateway to prematurely terminate the connection at the 120-second steady-state timeout and resulting in false HTTP 200 empty responses followed by client reconnect loops; introduced state-aware lifecycle tracking (`InitialTtft` -> `Thinking`/`Streaming` -> `Transition`) to `create_codex_sse_stream` and `create_legacy_sse_stream`, automatically promoting to the `Transition` phase upon steady-state silence, granting an independent `transition_secs` (180s) grace window, and streaming continuous `: ping\n\n` heartbeats to keep the downstream connection alive while upstream completes full buffer synthesis. (Fixes #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @mclinyeshi, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **First-Byte Budget Ceiling Expansion**: Raised `MAX_FIRST_BYTE_BUDGET_SECS` from 150s to 180s, preventing massive-context prefill requests under peak upstream load from exhausting the initial byte budget too early and triggering unnecessary fallback retries culminating in 504 Gateway Timeout. (Fixes #3816, PR #3817, Thanks to @mclinyeshi, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Regression Test Coverage**: Added the `test_codex_stream_survives_transition_cliff_after_thinking` unit test, simulating post-reasoning monolithic tool buffering to verify that the stream survives the silence gap and delivers `response.completed` cleanly. (PR #3817, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.8 (2026-11-02)**:
        -   **[3P Quota Decoupling and Unauthorized Slot Blanks, Eliminating Phantom Quotas] (Fixes #3809, Ref #3811, PR #3812, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Capability-Gated Frontend Blanks**: Added actual model authorization checks to `getGroupQuotaSummaries`; accounts with no usable Claude / 3P models now return empty values so quota cards render the standard `-` placeholder, removing the false 100% saturation caused by idle upstream 3P quota groups; dashboard averages, bottleneck extraction, and the 5H best-account ranking now skip accounts without 3P capability, safely showing `-` instead of errors when no account qualifies. (Fixes #3809, PR #3812, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(Fixes #3809, Ref #3811, PR #3812, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Regression Coverage**: Added the frontend `claudeQuotaSanitize` suite and the Rust `test_update_quota_purges_phantom_3p_groups_when_no_claude_or_gpt_models` test covering blanks without 3P models, normal listings, and fully disabled model isolation.         -   **[Inbound Model Gate Documentation Alignment and Ordering Regression Test] (Ref #3815)**:

(Ref #3815)
            -   **Locked Map-Before-Judge Ordering**: Added a regression test covering exact, dotted-version canonicalization, and wildcard alias paths to prevent future refactors from moving the retired check ahead of custom mapping and killing user aliases again. (Ref #3815)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.7 (2026-11-02)**:
        -   **[Gemini Native Stream Interruption Root-Cause Transparency & Premature EOF Diagnostics] (Fixes #3813)**:
            -   **Official Standard finishMessage Diagnostic Forwarding**: Resolved the issue where native Gemini streams encountering connection drops (e.g. `error reading a body from connection` or idle timeouts) after partial output emitted a synthesized recovery chunk with only a hardcoded `MAX_TOKENS`, masking the true network failure and misleading downstream clients into diagnosing an output token limit exceeded; integrated the error report into candidate frames using Google's official standard `finishMessage` alongside structured `gatewayError` and `gatewayInterrupted: true` flags, preserving all generated content cleanly under the zero content tampering contract while exposing exact interruption details. (Fixes #3813)
            -   **Incomplete Premature EOF Fallback**: Fixed an issue where streams emitting content but closed prematurely by upstream without an official `finishReason: "STOP"` resulted in a silent EOF without completion metadata; added an incomplete stream guard in the finalize hook to automatically emit a diagnostic chunk explaining the premature EOF, while preserving silent EOF when valid completion markers are present to protect trailing usage metadata chunks. (Fixes #3813)
        -   **[Token Stats Dual-Perspective Redesign, Multi-Dashboard TTFT Integration, and Minimalist Control Bar Upgrade] (Fixes #3810)**:
            -   **Dual-Perspective Model Breakdown**: Resolved the issue where token statistics recorded only the client's original requested model, masking heavy consumption by routed premium models (e.g., Fable 5, Opus); introduced `effective_model()` on `ProxyRequestLog` to split token tracking into "Routed Model" (actual quota/cost consumption) and "Requested Model" (original client call) perspectives; User Token tracking is aligned with the effective model simultaneously. (Fixes #3810)
            -   **Multi-Dashboard Time to First Token (TTFT) Integration**: Added `ttft_ms REAL` to `token_usage` table to accurately capture millisecond-precision response latency for both streaming首包 and non-streaming responses; seamlessly integrated into all dashboards: **Cyber Cockpit View** displays real-time global TTFT on the efficiency dial and model TTFT tags on model rank cards; **Apple Bento View** shows average TTFT on cache efficiency cards and top models list; **Classic View** introduces a dedicated "TTFT" metric card and an "TTFT" column in the detailed model statistics table.
            -   **Backward-Compatible DB Schema & Aggregation API**: Dynamically added `requested_model` and `ttft_ms` fields with dedicated indexes to the `token_usage` table; extended `get_token_stats_by_model` and model trend endpoints with the `view_mode` parameter, leveraging dynamic `COALESCE(NULLIF(requested_model, ''), model)` aggregation to keep both perspectives clean and fully backward compatible. (Fixes #3810)
            -   **Historical Data Inference Migration & Discrepancy Notice**: Runs an automatic one-time migration upon startup for historical records lacking `requested_model`, backfilling the calculated routed target into `model` and preserving the legacy record as `requested_model`. **Note: Because raw request logs are periodically rotated, historical records are migrated by inferring routing targets from current default routing rules; past requests that utilized customized overrides or multi-stage fallback rotations may reflect minor attribution discrepancies**, whereas newly dispatched requests are tracked with real-time exactness. (Fixes #3810)
            -   **Daily Stats Window Alignment & Data Clarity**: Fixed an issue where the "Day" filter internally defaulted to a 14-day window, mistakenly blending historical older usage into daily counts; aligned "Day" strictly with the last 24 hours / today, paired with contextual tooltips on all time ranges to prevent period mismatch and data confusion.
            -   **Minimalist Control Bar Redesign & Vertical Space Recovery**: Removed the redundant, oversized "Token Usage Statistics" header to eliminate visual duplication with the active global nav tab and reclaim substantial vertical screen real estate; seamlessly embedded minute-level granular switches (`1h / 24h`) directly inside the "Minute" pill button; neatly grouped dashboard view toggles, model perspectives, time spans, custom pricing, and refresh actions into a unified single-line control bar without awkward multi-row wrapping.
            -   **Full 12-Locale Multi-Language Localization**: Completed translations and tooltips across all 12 supported system languages covering routed/requested perspectives, TTFT latency, and time-range descriptors. (Fixes #3810)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.6 (2026-11-01)**:
        -   **[Account Deletion Consistency Fix: Deleted Accounts Are No Longer Dispatched, with Zero-Side-Effect Loading] (Fixes #3806, PR #3807, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)**:
            -   **Index-Driven Pool Admission**: The proxy pool now treats `accounts.json` as the single admission source; credential files missing from the index are logged and skipped instead of being loaded again, completely eliminating ghost accounts (deleted in the UI but still on disk) from the rotation that caused 503 responses and `Cross-account replay cap reached` on large payloads. (Fixes #3806, PR #3807, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)
            -   **Zero-Side-Effect Loading Path**: Orphan files are no longer physically deleted while loading accounts, preventing irreversible refresh-token loss during the legitimate "file written but index not yet committed" window (streaming batch import) or when only the `accounts/` directory is restored from a backup; orphan detection now matches both the file stem and the internal `id` so historical files with `stem != id` are not misclassified. (PR #3807, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)
            -   **Hot Reload and Deletion Flow Hardening**: `reload_account()` now only unloads accounts missing from the index and returns success, avoiding false errors surfaced by enable/disable toggles and quota refreshes; `delete_account()` moved to fail-before-the-point-of-no-return (index persisted first, then the credential file removed, with errors propagated) while keeping orphan fallback cleanup; bulk deletion now hot-reloads the proxy pool immediately, keeping Web/CLI and desktop behavior aligned. (PR #3807, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)
            -   **Regression Coverage**: Added and adjusted unit tests covering both final states — orphan files preserved on disk but excluded from the proxy pool, and orphan files still cleanable through the explicit delete API. (PR #3807, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @dnmdngcsb)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.5 (2026-11-01)**:
        -   **[Web/Docker Mode OAuth Flow Fixes, State Tearing Protection, and Synchronous Exchange Feedback] (Fixes #3802, PR #3803, PR #3805, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **State Tearing & Duplicate Prepare Prevention**: Optimized `handleOAuthWeb` in `AddAccountDialog` to prioritize reusing already prepared authorization URLs rather than re-triggering `prepare_oauth_url` upon clicking "Start OAuth"; added idempotent state reuse in backend `prepare_oauth_flow_manually` (returning existing URLs for identical client and redirect_uri), completely eliminating state overrides that caused callback codes to drop into cancelled channels. (Fixes #3802, PR #3803, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Built-in Client Loopback Fallback Guard (PR #3803)**: Added an `is_builtin_oauth_client` guard in `resolve_oauth_redirect_uri` that enforces `http://localhost:<port>/auth/callback` for the default built-in Google client even if `ABV_PUBLIC_URL` is set, completely avoiding upstream Google 400 `redirect_uri_mismatch` errors caused by unauthorized custom domains. (PR #3803, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Active Flow Cleanup on Exchange Failure (PR #3805)**: Added explicit `cancel_oauth_flow()` calls upon exchange errors in both `submit_oauth_code` and HTTP `handle_oauth_callback`, immediately freeing the global single-flow state when Google returns `invalid_grant` or expired codes; additionally clears the frontend `oauthUrl` cache upon submission error, completely eliminating stale flow state reuse and retry deadlocks. (PR #3805, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Bi-directional CSRF Fallback & Resilient Manual Input Parsing (PR #3803, PR #3805)**: Automatically extracts and forwards the original `state` from `oauthUrl` as a fallback when submitting manual codes in the frontend, preventing CSRF verification bypasses on bare code inputs; upgraded `parse_manual_oauth_input` to reliably handle full URLs, schemeless `localhost:<port>/...` addresses, `%2F` encoded strings, raw codes, and upstream `error=access_denied` callbacks. (Fixes #3802, PR #3803, PR #3805, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Client Key Preservation & Account Pool Sync**: Replaced the detached background `tokio::spawn` task in Web mode; manual code submissions and web callbacks now strictly preserve and forward the bound `client_key` to `exchange_code_with_client`, accompanied by active `token_manager.load_accounts()` reloading on callback, eliminating upstream `invalid_grant: Bad Request` errors caused by client mismatch. (Fixes #3802, PR #3803, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Headless/Docker Deployment Contextual Guidance & Action Guards**: Added a clear contextual callout above the manual code input in Web mode explaining that browser connection refusal upon redirecting to `localhost` is normal Google Desktop OAuth behavior, guiding remote users to copy and paste the full callback URL from the address bar; restricted the "I have authorized, continue" button to desktop mode to prevent Web UI confusion. (Fixes #3802, PR #3803, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520, @cubelikeplayDaniel)

            -   **Frontend Command Mapping Parity**: Aligned route mappings in `src/utils/request.ts` (`COMMAND_MAPPING`), ensuring Web UI users receive official Hub update notifications upon launch and can actively inspect and detect Hub fingerprints in the Settings panel. (Ref #3797)

(Fixes #3801, Thanks to @dandelion-promise520)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.3 (2026-11-01)**:
        -   **[Weekly Quota "Pedal Mode" with Auto Circuit-Breaker Recovery] (Fixes #3795, PR #3796, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Expiring Quota Detection & Prompt**: Independently monitors weekly quotas for Gemini and Claude in the account pool, prompting a reminder dialog when remaining quota exceeds 5% with less than 24 hours until the official reset; supports a persistent "Don't remind me again" choice per model family, plus a 2-hour silent cooldown after "Not now" to avoid repeated interruptions. (Fixes #3795, PR #3796, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Snapshot-Based Suspension & Precise Restoration**: Creates a pre-activation state snapshot and temporarily suspends all non-target accounts (`pedal_mode_locked`) so traffic concentrates on the expiring accounts; on exit, only the accounts suspended by this session are restored, while accounts the user had manually disabled remain untouched, preventing pool state corruption. (Fixes #3795, PR #3796, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Exhaustion-Only Safe Exit & Deadlock Guards**: Exits automatically only when every target account drops to 0% or the official reset time has passed; fixed a deadlock where the negative countdown after the reset time blocked exit, excluded user-disabled accounts from being selected as targets, added fallback restoration when a target account is deleted, and applied conservative guards against quota-loading fluctuation to prevent a premature exit. (Fixes #3795, PR #3796, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Top Banner & Manual Exit**: Added a contextual status banner to the main UI header showing the active model, suspended account count, and accounts pending consumption, with manual exit and restore at any time; fully localized in Chinese and English with 9 automated edge-case unit tests. (Fixes #3795, PR #3796, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Claude Code 2.1.312 New-Generation Model Mapping and Pool-Adaptive Dark Routing] (Fixes #2065, Ref #1724, Ref #3731, Ref #3751, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Static Hardcoded Mapping Removed**: Deleted the `CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI` static hardcoded mapping, eliminating the deadlock risk of the entire Claude chain collapsing when a target model is retired or renamed. (Fixes #2065, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Adaptive Dark Routing (`auto:*`)**: Added `auto:gemini-flash-low`, `auto:claude-opus`, and `auto:claude-opus-4-7` pool-adaptive targets that choose the best available model among Opus 5.5 / 4.6 / 4.7 and other Claude thinking models based on real-time positive quota, preventing 502 when the whole pool is exhausted; `claude-haiku-*` now reuses `pick_background_model_from_pool` to dynamically select a lightweight shared pool with zero Claude coding quota drain. (Ref #1724, Ref #3731, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Public/Dark Exposure Toggle & Anti-Resurrection Migration**: Decoupled `custom_mapping` from `custom_mapping_expose`, keeping the three built-in compatibility rules hidden by default; introduced `builtin_mapping_version` for seamless migration so built-in rules survive a one-click reset; the mapping panel now supports public/dark state toggling and one-click reset to built-in adaptive routes. (Fixes #2065, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Gate Baseline & Thinking-Tier Passthrough**: Extracted the shared `parse_claude_version_tuple` helper to remove fragile duplicate version parsing; the Claude hard gate remains 4.6 (Fable / Mythos require ≥ 5.0, Haiku ≥ 4.5), and adaptive targets are resolved before the gate so dark routes are not falsely rejected as retired; the router now passes client thinking tiers through when a custom mapping target is a bare model. (Ref #3751, PR #3787, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Graceful Finalization for Abnormal Stream Drops with Zero Body Tampering] (PR #3794, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **No Content Loss on Mid-Stream Drops**: Refactored the shared `stream_lifecycle` state machine to distinguish "interruption after content/tool calls were emitted" from "first-packet or pure-thinking failure"; network jitter, connection resets, and idle timeouts now trigger graceful finalization with protocol-specific truncation terminals (Claude `max_tokens` / OpenAI `length` / Gemini `MAX_TOKENS`), preventing clients from throwing `StreamTruncatedError` and discarding tens of thousands of already-generated tokens. (PR #3794, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Zero Body Tampering & Tool-Call Priority**: Removed fabricated Markdown banners and unclosed code-fence injection to keep model output untouched, eliminating non-streaming and thinking-store contamination; tool-call semantics take absolute priority, so abnormal finalization strictly preserves `tool_use` / `tool_calls` and never rewrites them into truncation reasons that deadlock the next turn with HTTP 400. (PR #3794, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Terminal & Empty-Content Guards**: Interruptions during pure thinking or before any content/tool call was emitted still propagate errors downstream, never faking a finish or an empty answer; protocol handlers now expose `has_emitted_done` and other terminal awareness to prevent double-finalization frames from poisoning the client. (PR #3794, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Gemini Normal-EOF and Null-Upstream Fixes**: Removed the `GeminiStreamHandler::is_terminal` override so normal EOF streams no longer skip image-generation success accounting and model rate-limit cleanup; strengthened `finishReason` null checks so occasional upstream `null` fields do not cause misjudgment, with a new unit test asserting a normal EOF reaches `emit_finalize`. (Ref #3794, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.2 (2026-10-31)**:
        -   **[Multi-Agent Root Session Normalization and Key-Level Affinity Scheduling] (Fixes #3789, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)**:
            -   **Root Session Suffix Normalization**: Solves the 100% Prompt Cache miss issue caused by multi-agent frameworks (such as Cindy, AutoGPT, LangGraph) frequently deriving transient sub-session UUIDs during plan steps. Implemented `extract_root_session_id` within `SessionScope::resolve` to automatically strip derived suffixes (`:step:N`, `:subtask:...`, `:agent:...`, `:talk:...`, `--step-N`, `::`); the affinity routing key converges to the root session to lock the upstream account and restore sub-second TTFT caching, while the store key preserves the complete sub-session ID for isolated thinking storage. (Fixes #3789, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
            -   **Credential-Level Affinity (AffinityScope::ApiKey)**: Extended `StickySessionConfig` with an affinity scope toggle, supporting seamless switching between `session` (default) and `api_key` modes to lock all requests under the same API token to a single account with automatic failover on 429 or quota exhaustion; supported headless override via `ANTIGRAVITY_SCHEDULING_AFFINITY_SCOPE`. (Fixes #3789)
            -   **Contextual UI Pill Toggle & 12-Locale Support**: Added an intuitive pill toggle in the Scheduling configuration panel within ApiProxy settings, fully localized across all 12 supported languages. (Fixes #3789)
        -   **[Remember Login Credentials in Web/Docker Mode] (Fixes #3791, PR #3792, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Optional Credential Persistence**: Added an optional "Remember login credentials" checkbox to the Web/Docker login dialog, disabled by default to maintain shared device security; persists API Key to `localStorage` upon successful authentication, eliminating redundant re-logins on browser restart. (Fixes #3791, PR #3792, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Request Fallback & 401 Listener Lifecycle Fix**: `request.ts` now prioritizes `sessionStorage` with graceful fallback to `localStorage`; resolved an existing lifecycle defect where active sessions returned prematurely and skipped registering the global `abv-unauthorized` listener. (Fixes #3791, PR #3792, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Stage Timeout Overview and Diagnostics Refinements]**:
            -   **Clear Descriptions & Grace Semantics**: Enhanced contextual hints and documentation for the 3-stage stream lifecycle timeout state machine, clarifying transition cliff grace windows and sliding idle timeouts.

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.1 (2026-10-30)**:
        -   **[Large Payload Upload Stall Decoupling and Thinking QoS False Positive Fix] (Ref #3763, Fixes #3772, Ref #3768, Ref #3781, PR #3784, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Upload Stall Decoupling & Floor Guard**: Completely decoupled `stall_secs` from adaptive `upload_budget_secs`, ensuring stall detection never shrinks to 3~5s and strictly respects `max connect_secs` to avoid false stall aborts during TLS/proxy handshakes; enforced a 15s budget floor for payloads ≥256KB. (Ref #3763, PR #3784, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Upload Terminal State & Replay Cascade Suppression**: Explicitly marked `UpstreamTerminal::Upload` terminal state when all primary and fallback endpoints fail during upload, immediately returning 504 to prevent blind cross-account retries from exhausting healthy account quotas. (Ref #3763, PR #3784, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Unified Pipeline-Level Thinking Recognition**: Implemented `payload_enables_thinking` under `pipeline::qos` to inspect converted `generationConfig.thinkingConfig` payloads directly while matching `argon`, `-high`, `o1/o3/o4`, and `v4.0+` patterns, fundamentally preventing thinking models like `gemini-3.8-flash-high` from being misjudged with a 20s non-thinking threshold during multi-turn chats. (Fixes #3772, Ref #3768, PR #3784, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Adaptive Security Prompt Sanitization**: Expanded `RE_SECURITY_CTF_POLICY` to seamlessly match security instructions missing the `authorized` prefix, extending fast-path pre-filters and multi-turn user prompt unit tests. (Ref #3781, PR #3784)

    *   **v5.0.2-beta.0 (2026-10-30)**:
        -   **[Dedicated QoS Auto-Quarantine Toggle and Duration Check Bypass] (Fixes #3783, Thanks to @TakimotoUki)**:
            -   **Manual Control & Disabled by Default**: Added the `proxy.qos_auto_jail` configuration field, defaulted to disabled (`false`). In high-jitter network environments, users have full control to prevent accounts from being quarantined due to transient network stalls. (Fixes #3783, Thanks to @TakimotoUki)
            -   **Duration Check Bypass & Instant Unjail**: When disabled, incoming TTFT durations are ignored during QoS evaluation—no suspicious tags are assigned, accounts are never jailed, and sticky sessions are preserved; toggling the switch from enabled to disabled immediately unjails and restores all restricted accounts to Healthy in memory. (Fixes #3783)
            -   **Dashboard Quick Toggle & Full 12-Locale Support**: Introduced an intuitive pill toggle on the Dashboard header (adjacent to the available accounts filter) with dynamic badge states, instantaneous hot-reload, and contextual tooltips; aligned Settings UI and fully localized across all 12 supported languages. (Fixes #3783)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.9 (2026-10-29)**:
        -   **[Pipeline Convergence: Unified Terminal Handling, Auto-Heal, Budgets, Routing, Rotation, and Observability] (Ref #3763, #3751, #3735)**:
            -   **Unified Four-Protocol Timeouts & Sunk Global Budget**: Timeout policies and the 270s global request budget across all four protocols (Google Responses / Chat Completions / Claude / Gemini) are now uniformly managed by the pipeline stage; requests with exhausted budget are short-circuited immediately to prevent redundant upstream rotation. (Ref #3763, #3735)
            -   **Stateful Rotation & Thinking Auto-Heal**: Unified empty thinking recovery and stream protocol error event recognition; stateful retry evaluation combined with scheduling-mode in-place waiting prevents Claude livelocks and account pool cascading failures. (Ref #3751, #3763)
            -   **Model Gate & Official Thinking Migration**: Aligned with the 2026-10-29 official thinking migration (`thinkingLevel` prioritized, `thinkingBudget` fallback, explicitly disabled thinking falls back to lowest tier); egress platform fingerprint calibrated to 2.28.0 / CL 1008300195; model lists, agent configs, and UI selectors now strictly expose available model sets, retiring all Gemini < 3 models. (Ref #3751, #3763)
            -   **Proactive Flush on Exit & Trace Timeline**: Active best-effort disk flush on exit; full-link stage timeline diagnostics and tracking for slow requests (≥25s). (Ref #3763)
        -   **[Endpoint Streaming Real TTFT Closed-Loop Backfill & Negative Value Guard] (Ref #3763, #3751)**:
            -   **Cross-Stage TTFT Probe Backfill**: Added the `StreamTtftCapture` container mounted via Response extensions; on stream completion, `monitor_middleware` backfills the genuine clean TTFT captured from the first valid data chunk into request logs, thoroughly replacing the `-1.000` header placeholder. (Ref #3763)
            -   **UI Defenses & Graceful Degradation**: `ProxyMonitor` and `RequestDetailModal` now filter out negative durations (`<= 0`) and fall back to effective endpoint attempt durations, resolving the `-` TTFT in tables and the `-0.0010s` (`-0.0%`) display glitch in details drawers. (Ref #3751, Ref #3763)
        -   **[Dynamic Inbound Sanitization for Client CTF and Security Policy Prompts] (Fixes #3781, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)**:
            -   **Adaptive Security Policy Stripping**: Strips Antigravity-injected CTF and defensive security instructions via regex with dual-anchor adaptation (preserving adjacent `# Harness` chapter headers or closing on `defensive use cases.`). (Fixes #3781, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
            -   **Code Block Protection & Empty Block Pruning**: Deep code block isolation (``` and `) ensures 100% exemption for user code; empty parts post-sanitization are physically purged, and empty `systemInstruction` objects are deregistered to prevent 400 Bad Request and avoid model refusal false positives. (Fixes #3781, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
        -   **[Antigravity IDE 2.6.0+ Account Switching Fix & Background Orphan Cleanup] (Fixes #3778, Fixes #3770, Ref #3762, PR #3780, Thanks to @lbjlaq)**:
            -   **Dual-Write Injection**: Implemented simultaneous dual-write for Antigravity IDE (2.6.0+) into SQLite `state.vscdb`, system Keyring (`gemini:antigravity`), and file fallbacks, resolving stale credential reads by the core `agy` language service. (Fixes #3778, Fixes #3770, PR #3780, Thanks to @lbjlaq)
            -   **Language Server Process Recognition**: Added `is_language_server_process` recognition inside `is_helper_process` and matched the `--hub` flag in `matches_target_client` to target `ide`. (PR #3780)
            -   **Orphan Process Cleanup**: Expanded cross-platform `close_antigravity` calls to `sweep_orphan_language_servers`, terminating orphan background `agy` processes during restart or account switching. (PR #3780, Ref #3762)
        -   **[Direct Account List Refresh upon QoS Jail Release] (Ref #3749, Ref #3768, PR #3782, Thanks to @wthsama)**:
            -   **Decoupled Release from Quota Refresh**: Releasing QoS jail now only resets local runtime QoS state instead of triggering remote `handleRefresh` / `refreshQuota` calls prone to 429 failures. (Ref #3768, PR #3782, Thanks to @wthsama)
            -   **Instant Local Feedback**: Directly calls `useAccountStore.getState().fetchAccounts()` to refresh the local account list, immediately clearing the jail badge and countdown while eliminating redundant quota queries in table views. (PR #3782, Thanks to @wthsama)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.8 (2026-10-29)**:
        -   **[Tool Signature Cache Read/Write Closed-Loop Optimization: Eliminating SQLite Write Amplification and Serialized Read Bottlenecks] (PR #3765, PR #3774, Ref #753, Thanks to @zepeng-jin, @jeikl)**:
            -   **Write-Path Deduplication & Capacity Alignment**: Raised L1 `TOOL_CACHE_LIMIT` from 500 to 10,000, fully aligned with the SQLite L2 table cap; `cache_tool_signature` now only refreshes the in-memory timestamp and skips redundant persistence when the same unexpired signature is already in L1, eliminating the 10–17s local CPU/disk stall caused by multi-turn history replay (a single request with 125 tool calls used to trigger 250 synchronous writes). (PR #3765, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Background Batch Writes & Stale-Signature Guard**: Added the `ag-tool-sig-writer` background thread (bounded queue + up to 512 `INSERT OR REPLACE` rows per transaction) backed by a monotonic `PERSIST_SEQ`, the `SESSION_MIN_PERSIST_SEQ` / `GLOBAL_MIN_PERSIST_SEQ` watermarks and `PENDING_SESSION_DELETES`, so stale saves are precisely dropped no matter how concurrent saves interleave with `delete_session_signature` / `invalidate_session`; writes reuse a persistent connection with `prepare_cached`, table pruning is throttled to every 128 writes or 5 minutes with a startup pre-prune to avoid first-write blocking. (PR #3765)
            -   **Batched Reads & Read-Only Connection Pool**: Added `get_tool_signatures_batch`; `finalize` prefetches all functionCall IDs for the turn with a single `IN` query and negative-caches misses. The read-only pool defaults to 4 round-robin connections, and L1 moved from a single `Mutex<HashMap>` to a sharded `DashMap`, removing global lock contention under high concurrency. (PR #3774)
            -   **Delete Priority & Environment-Adaptive Profiles**: Deletes now use a dedicated high-priority channel with O(1) pending lookup; saves rejected by a full queue enter a dirty set retried by idle workers, with a best-effort flush on Tauri exit. The new `CacheProfile` (Low / Standard / High / Server) derives L1 capacity, read pool, batch size, queue depth and retention rows from CPU, memory and deployment shape, all overridable via environment variables. (PR #3774)
            -   **Observability & Configuration Entry**: The thinking settings card now exposes read-only L1/L2 hit rate, batch calls/IDs, queue drops, pending deletes and L1 capacity, plus a "Clear signature cache" action (no raw SQLite knobs); compaction-history segments short-circuit pointless signature/thinking lookups. (PR #3765, PR #3774)
        -   **[First Response Header Folded into Unified TTFT Budget with Four-Protocol Guardrails] (PR #3775, Ref #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)**:
            -   **Unified First-Byte Budget**: The first-byte budget is now `max(user TTFT, token-adaptive estimate)` capped at 150s, plus media increments (text 0 / image +45 / video +60 / auxiliary −30) with the fallback endpoint receiving half; each request owns one budget shared across endpoints with one third reserved for fallbacks, eliminating the endpoint × account time multiplication that killed healthy large-context requests. (PR #3775, Ref #3763)
            -   **Terminal Replay Suppression**: Exhausting the first-byte budget no longer penalizes the account or replays the same body against another account — it returns 504 directly, ending the "the more it retries, the slower it gets" livelock and 5–10 minute client hangs. (PR #3775)
            -   **Global Budget Sunk into the Pipeline**: A 270s global budget (environment-overridable) now lives in the pipeline and applies to all four protocols, clamping every upstream call to the remaining budget so the global ceiling actually holds. (PR #3775)
        -   **[Interface & Font Scaling with Global Keyboard Shortcuts] (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)**:
            -   **Two-Layer Zoom Engine**: Desktop now prefers the native Tauri `webview.setZoom()` (WebView2 / WKWebView / WebKitGTK) for true viewport-wide proportional scaling, gracefully degrading to CSS `zoom` in web/browser mode and as a cold-start fallback; `index.html` preloads the stored zoom on the first frame to eliminate flicker. (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)
            -   **Shortcuts & Interaction Boundaries**: `Cmd/Ctrl + =` zoom in, `Cmd/Ctrl + -` zoom out and `Cmd/Ctrl + 0` reset, with a 1.2s auto-fading HUD near the top center; key repeats are ignored, `Alt` combos are excluded, editable inputs are protected, and the modifier is platform-aware (Cmd only on macOS, Ctrl elsewhere). (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)
            -   **Level Alignment & Window Isolation**: Settings → General gains an 85%–150% selector backed by `snapToClosestZoom`, so non-standard cached values snap to a valid level and boot/runtime stay consistent; the mini overlay is guarded in both the boot script and `applyZoom` to keep its native scale and avoid clipping the fixed 360×360 panel. (PR #3766)
            -   **Full Localization**: Added entries for all 12 locales (zh / zh-TW / en / ja / ko / es / pt / ru / tr / vi / ar / my), so non-Chinese UIs no longer fall back to Chinese. (PR #3766, Thanks to @nicolastinkl)

(Fixes #3770, PR #3771, Thanks to @fuyangtian2003-beep)

(Fixes #3770, PR #3771, Thanks to @fuyangtian2003-beep)

        -   **[Scrollbar Aesthetic Refinement and Hover-to-Show Interaction] (Fixes #3768, Thanks to @ddmixi)**:

(Fixes #3768, Thanks to @ddmixi)
