    *   **v5.0.1-beta.6 (2026-10-28)**:
        -   **[Inbound Model Gate & Punishment-Chain Exemptions; Fix 429/503 Collateral Damage to the Account Pool] (Ref #3764, Ref #3769, Thanks to @jeikl)**:
            -   **Four-Stage Model Gate**: Missing model → 404 `model not exist`; custom mappings take precedence (exact / case / normalized / wildcard) and the raw ID is preserved otherwise; the mapped ID must exist in the union of downstream-exposed models, the agent tool list, the official catalog and mapping values; if no account holds positive quota → 502 `no quota accounts`. Fails open when the pool has no model metadata yet, so a cold start cannot wipe out all traffic. (Ref #3764)
            -   **All Entry Points Covered**: Beyond POST generate / messages / chat (including Responses), the gate now also guards `:countTokens` colon syntax, `/v1beta/models/:model/countTokens`, and all four protocol GET probe endpoints: `/v1/models/:model`, `/v1/model/:model`, `/v1/responses/models/:model`, `/responses/models/:model`, `/v1/models/claude/:model`, `/v1beta/models/:model`. (Ref #3764)
            -   **Punishment Chain Fully Exempt**: `is_gateway_self_answer` is the single source of truth and is wired into `policy::classify` (highest priority → InternalGatewayMessage), rate-limit parsing, `mark_rate_limited(_fast/_async)`, `classify_rate_limit_reason` and `mark_account_transient_failure`. Gateway-generated 404/502 no longer lock accounts, unbind sticky sessions, or write zero-quota markers, eliminating collateral damage to innocent accounts. (Ref #3764)
            -   **Legacy Model Families Retired**: Claude 3.x / < 4.6 and Gemini 2.x are hard-blocked ahead of custom mappings; the `gemini-2.5-* → 3.1-flash-lite` internal alias, legacy entries in the OpenCode static catalog and `VariantType::Gemini25Thinking` were removed. Image-generation families are exempt and accept `-preview / -1k / -2k / -4k / aspect-ratio` variants plus version-synonym normalization. (Ref #3769)
            -   **Hardcoded Fallback Models Removed**: All three `INTERNAL_BACKGROUND_TASK` constants were deleted; background summary models are now derived from the live pool (newest `flash-low` → bare `flash` → skip). Scheduler warm-up fallbacks are pool-driven and skip when no flash model exists instead of falling back to a hardcoded ID. (Ref #3769)
            -   **Image Model Visibility & Quota Consistency**: The model list only registers a normalized base image model when an account holds positive real quota (covering `/v1/models`, `/v1beta/models` and the agent one-click config), and the quota gate counts same-family variant quota so a listed model never answers with 502. (Ref #3769)
            -   **Frontend Observability**: The traffic-log account column highlights `model not exist` (rose) and `no quota accounts` (amber) with high contrast in both light and dark themes, while the forwarded-payload panel shows an English remediation guide (detail + hint). (Ref #3764)
            -   **QoS Ladder Probe Fixed**: The probe now sends a complete official v1internal envelope (`project` / `requestId` / `request` / `model` / `userAgent`), picks a Flash-tier model from the account's real metadata, and falls back Daily → Sandbox. Measured 200 upstream (TTFT ≈ 1.0–1.9s), so jailed accounts can self-heal back to Healthy. (Ref #3769)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.5 (2026-10-28)**:
        -   **[Prompt Sanitization Split by Injection Region / Current Turn; User Content Fully Exempt] (Fixes #3764)**:
            -   **Current Turn Passed Through 100%**: Sanitization tracks are now System / Content, with a new Anchored track for the current turn — only the contents of `<system*>` / `<identity*>` tags are normalized; everything outside the tags is preserved byte-for-byte. Single-turn translation, proofreading, and code-audit requests are no longer rewritten into the official identity. (Fixes #3764)
            -   **Injection Boundary Keyed to the Last Text Part**: The injected region ends before the last text part of the final user message, which covers clients that put the system prompt in multiple leading parts as well as those appending images or files after text. Billing-metadata parts are dropped before scope selection so indices cannot drift and misclassify the current turn. (Fixes #3764)
            -   **Two-Stage Guard for Identity Declarations**: The bridge segment between noun phrase and attribution verb can no longer cross line breaks (preventing `You are ...` and a following `Based on ...` paragraph from being swallowed together), and the replacement callback now skips interrogative sentences and cross-clause conjunctions, keeping `你是基于什么模型开发的？` and `..., and it was created by ...` untouched. (Fixes #3764)
            -   **Idempotent Across Multiple Passes**: The sanitizer runs at the adapter, handler, and pre-send stages, and again on retries. An idempotency regression test protects `compute_prefix_hash` prefix stability and Prompt Caching. (Fixes #3764)
        -   **[Process Identity Detection Consolidated into a Single Entry Point; Antigravity IDE 2.6.0 Account Switch Fixed] (Fixes #3762, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Identity Only from Executable Name/Path**: Added `classify_process`; command-line arguments are demoted to an IDE-vs-classic discriminator and only the Chromium `--user-data-dir` value is read. The weak `(antigravity && vscode)` heuristic was removed, so VS Code, terminals, and Explorer windows that merely open an antigravity project are no longer swept into the `taskkill /F /T` list. (Fixes #3762)
            -   **IDE Targets Forced to SQLite Injection**: When the target is an IDE, credentials are always written to `state.vscdb` instead of falling back to the system Keyring merely because the version is >= 2.0.0, which previously forced a re-login; an explicit classic selection always wins. (Fixes #3762)
            -   **Manager Guard Restricted to File Names**: The loose branch of `is_manager_or_tools_process` now inspects only the file stem, fixing the silent failure where a client installed under `D:\Tools\Antigravity` or `C:\Tools\agy` was mistaken for the Manager (never detected, so account switching wrote credentials without restarting it). (Fixes #3762)
            -   **Orphan Language Servers Swept per Target**: `sweep_orphan_language_servers(target_ide)` now cleans up only the matching client, so IDE and classic instances never kill each other. (Fixes #3762, Ref #3769)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.4 (2026-10-28)**:
        -   **[Phased Adaptive Upload Timeouts & Unified Signature/Thinking Governance Across Four Protocols] (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)**:
            -   **Phased Timeouts & Upload Budget**: Split connect, upload, first-response-header, and large-file retry into independent stages. Upload budget is now derived as `request body size ÷ upload bandwidth × (1 + extra wait ratio)` (default 2 Mbps + 0.4), with a 45s stall timeout capped by the upload budget. Header waits are scheduled per media type (text 30/25s, image 45/25s, video 60/30s, other 15/15s). Large bodies default to a single endpoint attempt, suppressing fallback and cross-account re-upload amplification. (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Gemini Stream Lifecycle Unification**: Migrated Gemini native streaming to the shared `run_stream_lifecycle`, so all four protocols now share the 3s SSE heartbeat and the initial-TTFT / transition / sliding guardrails. Removed the handwritten 300s timeout and pre-header first-chunk peek; keepalive starts immediately after headers. (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Unified Signature Retry & Thinking Governance**: On 400 signature errors, all protocols now skip ThinkingStore hydration, strip every real signature, degrade thought blocks into plain text (preserving reasoning content), and backfill the official sentinel for Gemini tool calls. Added `untrusted_signatures` to finalize to avoid "drop text first, strip signature later". Removed Claude `Thinking → Text` and OpenAI request-body specializations. (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Self-Healing & Pre-Filter Alignment**: Separated the `tool_choice` conflict flag from the signature-retry flag; added Gemini tool_choice/thinking conflict self-healing and inbound signature pre-filtering. All four protocols now align on overload budget degradation, corrupted images, signature errors, and tool-choice conflicts. (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Configuration & Copy**: Simplified the frontend timeout card into connect/upload/first-header/first-token sections with seven timeout fields; every field supports `ANTIGRAVITY_TIMEOUT_*` environment overrides, with all 12 locales synchronized. (PR #3767, Fixes #3763, Thanks to @jeikl)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.3 (2026-10-27)**:
        -   **[Corrupted Image 400 In-Place Auto-Healing & Fingerprint Blacklist Defense] (PR #3759, Fixes #3758, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **In-Place Recovery & Retry on 400**: Resolved conversation deadlocks where clients sending broken or unprocessable images encountered `400 Unable to process input image`. The pipeline precisely classifies image processing errors, avoiding account lockout or affinity detachment to safeguard prefix cache, and seamlessly replaces unprocessable images with structured placeholders in-place before retrying immediately. (PR #3759, Fixes #3758, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Single-Image Blacklisting & Multi-Image False-Positive Guard**: When a request contains exactly 1 image, its SHA-256 fingerprint is recorded into the global blacklist to intercept client replays at ingress; multi-image requests degrade images in-place for immediate recovery while bypassing the persistent blacklist to prevent healthy diagrams from being falsely blocked. (PR #3759, Ref #3758, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **FIFO Bounded Eviction & Comprehensive Schema Compatibility**: Rebuilt the image blacklist with `ImageBlacklist` (HashSet + VecDeque, 2000-capacity limit) using O(1) FIFO eviction to prevent cache clearing stampedes; added a zero-image short-circuit guard in handlers and fully supported top-level `request` wrapping alongside `systemInstruction` / `system_instruction` dual conventions. (PR #3759, Ref #3758, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Image Generation Watchdog Extension & Unified Empty Response Diagnostics] (Fixes #3756, Ref #3756, Thanks to @jeikl)**:
            -   **Extended Watchdog & Budget**: Relaxed image generation (`is_image_gen`) watchdog timeouts in `upstream/client.rs` to 90s for primary endpoints, 45s for fallback endpoints, and 135s overall budget, eliminating false-positive 15s watchdog aborts on 4K high-resolution generation. (Fixes #3756, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Pipeline Empty Image Diagnostics & Refusal Terminal Handling**: Built a protocol-agnostic image response validator and diagnostic extractor (`extract_image_generation_diagnostic`). When upstream returns 200 without valid image data, it rejects fake successes, extracts `promptFeedback.blockReason`, `finishReason`, and refusal text, and promptly returns a terminal 400 without futile account switching. (Fixes #3756, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Multi-Protocol Concurrency Alignment**: Aligned delayed account commitment and scheduler concurrency controls across OpenAI, Gemini, Claude, and Codex protocols, eradicating adapter-level code divergence and compilation type flaws. (Fixes #3756, Ref #3756, Thanks to @jeikl)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.2 (2026-10-27)**:

(PR #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound)
            -   **Safe Chunk State Machine & Panic Elimination**: Thoroughly replaced unsupported regex lookaheads with a high-performance text-chunking state machine for parsing and updating `cordis.patch.yml`, eradicating runtime Regex panics on empty or existing configs; fixed backslash-omission flaws in status-detection regexes; aligned the default model to `gemini-3.8-flash-high` and dynamically reused gateway core models. (Ref #3761, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Headless Terminal CLI Synchronization**: Integrated `antigravity-tools sync [app] [--model <model>]` in the CLI handler, enabling headless and server environments to synchronize configs to `dsh`, `claude`, `codex`, `opencode`, and more with a single command. (PR #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound)
            -   **Card Interaction Alignment**: Removed the disabled block on sync buttons when CLI binaries are uninstalled, keeping sync buttons and model selectors permanently accessible for pre-configuration; decoupled sync indicators, view, and restore actions to reflect true file states with 12-language localization. (PR #3761, Ref #3761, Thanks to @wakeupnotfound, @jeikl)
        -   **[High-Tier Argon Model Capability Harmonization & Ultra 503 Fix] (Fixes #3750, Ref #3745, Thanks to @jeikl)**:

            -   **Ultra 503 Elimination & Quota Inheritance**: Refactored quota inheritance inside `quota.rs` and `token_manager.rs`, allowing Ultra accounts to inherit the Gemini general quota pool rather than being discarded due to missing dedicated Argon groups, permanently resolving Ultra scheduling 503 failures. (Fixes #3750, Ref #3745, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Badges & UI Alignment**: Unified `AccountCard`, `AccountTable`, and full-width weekly quota card rendering so Ultra accounts properly illuminate Argon badges and display accurate limits, backed by `argonCapability.test.ts` automated tests. (Fixes #3750, Thanks to @jeikl)
        -   **[Token Stats Zero-Usage Bar Elimination & Hit-Rate Floor Guard] (Fixes #3755, PR #3755, Thanks to @EricZhou05)**:

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.1 (2026-10-26)**:
        -   **[Linux GTK Tray Cross-Thread Safe Dispatch & Global _exit Kill-Switch Elimination] (Fixes #3757, Ref #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)**:
            -   **GTK Menu Main-Thread Marshalling**: Dispatched all `MenuItem`, `Menu`, and `tray.set_menu` operations inside `update_tray_menus` strictly onto the native OS UI thread via `app.run_on_main_thread`, short-circuiting when display loss is detected. This permanently eradicates Tokio worker cross-thread GTK mutation deadlocks and assertion crash `_exit(1)`. (Fixes #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **Universal Worker Thread _exit Isolation (Zero Group Kill)**: Upgraded `linux_display_guard`'s `_exit` hook so that unless an intentional user-initiated exit was requested, no unexpected exit from the main thread or any worker thread (Tokio, WebKit IPC, GIO) is permitted to trigger `SYS_exit_group`. All failing threads are isolated and terminated individually via `pthread_exit(NULL)`, strictly safeguarding the core port 8045 proxy daemon. (Fixes #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **MiniView Periodic Timer & High-Account IPC Debouncing**: Optimized single-account refresh flow in `MiniView` by removing redundant duplicate `fetchAccounts` calls and skipping interval timer execution while the window is hidden/in the background, substantially decreasing IPC data thrashing and memory usage under 500+ accounts. (Fixes #3757, Thanks to @hazerwin)
        -   **[Web/Headless Update Settings 400 Fix & Partial Patch Merge Governance] (Fixes #3754)**:
            -   **Dual-Shape Request Body Compatibility for Axum Admin API**: `POST /api/system/updates/save` now accepts both the `{"settings": {...}}` wrapper forwarded by the frontend `invoke` mapping and the flat JSON used by Headless/CLI clients, permanently fixing the deterministic 400 Bad Request (content-length: 0) when switching to the Beta channel or toggling auto-check in Web mode. (Fixes #3754)
            -   **Update Settings Now Apply as Partial Patches**: Introduced `UpdateSettingsPatch` and `patch_update_settings` so only explicitly provided fields are overwritten; omitted fields (`last_check_time`, `update_channel`) retain their persisted values. This root-cause fixes the silent channel reset to the build default when toggling auto-check, and the last-check timestamp being zeroed on every save. (Fixes #3754)
            -   **Error Propagation & Empty-Patch Guard**: Persistence failures now surface as 500 with an error body, and empty patches return 400 with the list of expected fields, eliminating the previous `let _ =` silent-success path. (Fixes #3754)
            -   **Unified Semantics Across Web and Desktop**: The Tauri command now uses the same patch semantics; the frontend drops the hardcoded `last_check_time: 0` and submits only `update_channel` when switching channels. (Fixes #3754)
            -   **Regression Test Coverage**: Added unit tests for wrapped/flat payload parsing, unknown-field empty-patch detection, patch field preservation, and empty-patch rejection. (Fixes #3754)

    *   **v5.0.1-beta.0 (2026-10-26)**:
        -   **[Linux X11/xrdp Remote Desktop Display Guard Hardening & Seamless Fallback] (Fixes #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)**:
            -   **Native Xlib Fatal IO Error Interception (XSetIOErrorHandler)**: Dynamically hooked X11's native `XSetIOErrorHandler` in `linux_display_guard`. When xrdp / VNC remote desktop client disconnects, resets, or suffers network socket broken pipes (EPIPE / ECONNRESET), the fatal XIO error is trapped immediately at the lowest level, safely terminating only the failing GUI thread while keeping the background Tokio runtime and core port 8045 proxy service 100% active and uninterrupted. (Fixes #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **GLib Debug Log Pass-Through & Exhaustive X11 Pattern Matching**: Automatically exported `G_MESSAGES_DEBUG` to prevent GLib from silently discarding `g_debug` messages emitted by GTK 3.24+ `gdk_x_io_error`; comprehensively updated `is_display_disconnect_message` to recognize X11 fatal patterns including `Fatal IO error`, `on X server`, `X connection to`, and `Resource temporarily unavailable (errno 11)`. (Fixes #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **_exit Interceptor Main-Thread Isolation & Exit-Intent Recognition**: Recorded main GUI thread ID and introduced `USER_INITIATED_EXIT` tracking to strictly distinguish intentional user exits from graphics crashes, eradicating the flaw where unhandled display disconnects triggered `SYS_exit_group` and killed the proxy daemon. Remote desktop users experience seamless persistence without service drops. (Fixes #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)
            -   **Remote Virtual Desktop (xrdp/VNC) Safe Software Compositing Adaptation (Root Cause Eradication)**: Added virtual desktop environment heuristics in `linux_graphics.rs`. When running under xrdp / VNC sessions, automatically sets `WEBKIT_DISABLE_COMPOSITING_MODE=1`, `WEBKIT_DISABLE_DMABUF_RENDERER=1`, and software rasterization to root-cause eliminate WebKit accelerated compositor swap-buffer failures on client disconnection. The GUI window remains alive across disconnects while physical Linux desktops retain full GPU hardware acceleration without degradation. (Fixes #3752, Thanks to @hazerwin)

    *   **v5.0.0 (2026-10-24)**:
        -   **[Adaptive QoS Scheduling System via Pure Peer Calibration & Ladder Probing] (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @lbjlaq)**:
            -   **Real-time Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) Metric**: Captured pure elapsed time from request dispatch to the first effective chunk, completely eliminating noise from output length or streaming transfer to serve as the physical benchmark for silent QoS throttling. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **5-Tier Pure Peer Baseline & Anti-False-Positive Shield**: Peer baselines strictly exclude: self, heterogeneous tiers, disabled accounts, 429 rate-limited/zero-quota accounts, Jailed, Probing, Suspicious, and Half-Open accounts to prevent baseline poisoning; thinking models receive a dynamic 35s latency compensation to prevent false positives during deep reasoning; global outage brake halts strikes when the peer average exceeds 20s, coupled with an 8s~20s hysteresis deadband to prevent oscillation. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Transient Tagging & Immediate Session Handover**: Tags account as `Suspicious` on the first strike (instantly excluded from peer calculations, with the scheduler activating drain mode); 2 strikes within a 5-minute window isolates the account in jail and triggers immediate sticky session unbinding, allowing P2C to rebind healthy accounts with 100% Prompt Cache efficiency. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Background Ladder Probing & Half-Open Anti-Flapping**: Cooldown ladders scale at 2m -> 5m -> 10m -> 15m -> 20m -> 30m, resetting the timer at each step; dispatches lightweight synthetic "Hello" probes (< 5 tokens, 8s hard abort timeout); passing probes triggers `Half-Open` probation requiring 2 consecutive real requests under 8s before full release; 30-minute penalty memory retention prevents rapid looping. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **One-Click QoS Jail Release & Remaining Cooldown Display**: Displayed real-time QoS cooldowns on cards, tables, and monitor logs, providing an instant manual release button and backend API (`release_account_qos_jail`) across all 12 supported locale catalogs. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725, Thanks to @jeikl)
        -   **[Official Model Suite Upgrade, Tiered Thinking Protocol & Argon Standalone Quota Isolation] (Fixes #3735, Ref #3720, Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch)**:
            -   **Decoupled Model Ingestion from quotaInfo**: Completely eliminated the legacy `if let Some(quota_info) = info.quota_info` filter gate in `quota.rs`, benchmarking exclusively against official model catalogs to preserve Gemini models (`gemini-3.8-flash-*`, `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, etc.) when upstream omits model-level `quotaInfo`, permanently resolving local 503 scheduling rejections. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @xiaozhi-xmu, @chenshin166-arch, @jeikl)
            -   **Tiered Thinking Protocol (Thinking Budget vs Thinking Level)**: Governed new-generation tiered models (Gemini 4 Argon, Claude 5.5) exclusively with `thinkingLevel` (`"LOW"`, `"MEDIUM"`, `"HIGH"`) and traditional models with `thinkingBudget`, eliminating HTTP 400 dual-field conflicts. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Disabled Model Variant Ingestion Filtering & False Quota Elimination**: Ingested the `disabled: Option<bool>` field and `is_disabled()` helper in `OfficialModelInfo`, intercepting `-disabled` / `_disabled` suffixes at the top of the baseline check and stripping them from `normalize_to_standard_id` and the account pool; purged false badges and progress bars from frontend cards. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Gemini 4 Argon Dispatch Closure & Standalone Quota Inheritance**: Extended candidate filtering with `token_supports_advanced_model` and standalone bucket checks (`normalized_target != "claude" && token.model_quotas.contains_key(...)`) supporting camelCase and snake_case properties, eliminating phase-two 503 rejections while preserving Claude 5.5 / 4.6 pool separation. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Deprecate Obsolete gemini-pro-agent Codename**: Standardized `gemini-3.1-pro` mappings in `variant_mapping.rs` from deprecated internal codename `gemini-pro-agent` to official physical model `gemini-3.1-pro-high`. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Claude Parity for Dynamic Model Candidate Rewriting**: Integrated `token_manager.resolve_dynamic_model_for_account` into the Claude handler post-scheduling to match OpenAI and Gemini parity for adaptive per-account model rewriting. (Fixes #3737, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Standardized Context Window & Output Limits**: Standardized context and output limits across OpenAI (`/v1/models`), Claude (`/v1/models`), and Gemini (`/v1beta/models`) metadata endpoints. (Fixes #3735, Thanks to @jeikl)
        -   **[OpenAI Responses API Structured Outputs, Pipeline Tool Conflict Mitigation & Zero-Loss Unwrapping] (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel, @jeikl)**:
            -   **Inbound Protocol Normalization**: Supported `text.format` specifications in `OpenAIRequest` for the Responses API, extracted `effective_response_format` in request mapping, and normalized it into Gemini native `generationConfig.responseSchema` and `responseMimeType: "application/json"`, unblocking Codex Desktop's automatic thread naming (`thread_title`) and summary generation. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Background Thinking Budget Convergence & Timeout Prevention**: Explicitly honored `reasoning.effort: "low"` to constrain the thinking budget to 1024 tokens for background short tasks, eliminating 30-second hard timeout aborts by the Codex client during thread title generation on high-effort models. (Fixes #3710, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Unified Tool Conflict Mitigation in Pipeline (Pipeline First)**: Moved the mutual exclusivity handling of `responseSchema` and `tools` / `toolConfig` down to `align_google_request_prefix_topology_with_model` in `src/proxy/pipeline/inbound.rs`, suppressing `canonical_tools` and `canonical_tool_config` across all protocols when `responseSchema` is active to eliminate upstream HTTP 400 rejections. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Guarded Outer Unwrap & Zero-Loss Conversational Protection**: Guarded `convert_chat_response_to_responses` with `is_structured_output` to preserve 100% of conversational commentary in normal turns (preventing conversational data loss), while applying `unwrap_outer_code_fence_if_structured` strictly when the entire response is enclosed in markdown fences to unblock Codex Desktop's native `JSON.parse` without corrupting embedded code blocks. (Fixes #3710, Ref #3723, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Mini Floating Window Native macOS Transparency, Bidirectional Millisecond Sync & Deadlock-Free Scheduling] (Fixes #3733, Ref #3718, #3721, Ref #3738, Ref #3741, Thanks to @lbjlaq, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @jeikl)**:
            -   **Native macOS Transparency & Window Drag Permissions**: Added Tauri `macos-private-api` and native `macOSPrivateApi: true` configuration, eliminating WebKit black background edges and bringing native frosted glass transparency; injected transparency scripts on the first frame and declared drag/always-on-top capabilities. (Fixes #3733, Ref #3718, PR #3738, Thanks to @lbjlaq)
            -   **Dark/Light Adaptation & Cross-Window Millisecond i18n Sync**: Mini view dynamically inherits theme changes; bound `app://theme-changed` and `app://language-changed` cross-window events for bidirectional seamless synchronization across all 12 localization files. (Fixes #3733, PR #3738, Thanks to @lbjlaq)
            -   **Native Main Thread Scheduling & Win32 Deadlock Elimination**: Introduced `switch_to_main_window` and `hide_mini_view` commands in the Rust backend, dispatching main window reconstruction and reactivation (`exit_lightweight_mode`) fully onto the OS native UI main thread (`app.run_on_main_thread`), completely eliminating Win32 message loop deadlocks and spinlock hangs when creating windows cross-thread on Windows. (Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Ref #3741, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Mini Window React Router 404 Fallback & Synchronous Resolution**: Enhanced synchronous resolution in `App.tsx` using `window.location.search` (`?mini=1`) and `__TAURI_INTERNALS__` to eliminate routing re-entry caused by asynchronous IPC window label delays; supplemented `createBrowserRouter` with `errorElement` and `index.html` wildcard fallbacks. (PR #3741, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Lightweight Mode Seamless Fade-in on Render Ready**: Kept main window invisible by default (`visible: false`) during reconstruction, fading in and focusing smoothly only after ThemeManager mounts theme and configurations, completely eliminating black screen/white frame flickers before WebView2 paints the React DOM. (Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Thanks to @jeikl)
        -   **[Cross-Model Signature Architecture Refactor: Aligning with Official Antigravity Sentinel Discipline & Eliminating Claude Concurrent Tool 400 Errors] (Thanks to @jeikl, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Official Base64 Sentinel Parity**: Standardized the global sentinel constant from plaintext to the official 44-byte Base64 ciphertext `c2tpcF90aG91Z2h0X3NpZ25hdHVyZV92YWxpZGF0b3I=`; retained backward-compatible recognition for legacy plaintext sentinels (`LEGACY_SENTINEL_SIGNATURE`) to guarantee zero regression on cached turns. (Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Claude Zero-Sentinel & Concurrent Tool Cleanliness**: Fully exempted Claude models from synthetic sentinel injection; in concurrent tool use scenarios, signatures exclusively anchor on the first non-thought part (first tool or prose), while all subsequent concurrent tools strictly omit `thoughtSignature`, permanently eliminating `Invalid signature in thinking block` 400 rejections. (Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Claude ➔ Gemini Transition Foreign Tool Passports**: When switching from Claude back to Gemini, the inbound Gatekeeper automatically detects foreign Claude tools carrying the `toolu_` prefix and equips every foreign `functionCall` with a Base64 sentinel passport for seamless Gemini engine verification bypass, while strictly keeping prose parts pure without sentinels. (Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Gemini ➔ Claude Transition History Sanitization & Zero-Persistence**: When switching to Claude, the inbound pipeline completely purges legacy Gemini short signatures and sentinels; enforced strict guards in `normalize_and_heal_signature` so neither plaintext nor Base64 sentinels ever persist into SQLite storage. (Thanks to @jeikl)
        -   **[Streaming Giant Tool Call Grace Period Transition, Dynamic Timeout Budget Convergence & Claude 429 Elimination] (Fixes #3739, Fixes #3713, Ref #3506, Ref #3525, Ref #3720, PR #3740, PR #3714, PR #3742, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Streaming Giant Tool Call Buffer Transition**: Relaxed the cliff transition condition to `matches!(phase, StreamPhase::Thinking | StreamPhase::Streaming)`, automatically transitioning to StreamPhase::Transition with a 180s grace period when generating 10,000+ tokens giant `functionCall` payloads, preventing premature termination at 120s. (Fixes #3739, PR #3740, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **TTFT Header Watchdog & Total Budget Convergence**: Tightened primary streaming response header watchdog to a 30s base (elastically extended up to 45s for large payloads) with a 25s fallback margin, bounding total fallback budget to 55~70s (30s for non-streaming) and eliminating 85s~140s hangs on congested endpoints. (Fixes #3713, PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Integrated FailureStatusTracker into Claude Protocol**: Fully integrated `FailureStatusTracker` into `claude.rs` retry loops, recording `GATEWAY_TIMEOUT` on network errors and downgrading final status to 504 Gateway Timeout unless all attempts were genuine 429s, eliminating 64-second client backoff freezes. (PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Refactored Multi-Day Hard Quota Exhaustion Detection**: Accurately identified responses with `quotaResetDelay > 30min` (e.g., 68h) as account-level hard quota exhaustion, triggering 50ms rapid rotation in multi-account pools; applied 30s cooldown and unbinding upon full endpoint network errors. (PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Claude Thinking Budget Official Default Fallback**: In `resolve_custom_budget`, ensured Claude models return `None` when `claude_budget` is `<= 0`, enabling `InboundThinkingPipeline` to retrieve the authoritative default budget from `official_info.thinking_budget` (e.g. 1024). (Ref #3735, PR #3742, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)
            -   **Full-Quota Persistence on Import & Cold-Start Lock Decoupling**: Concurrently fetched tokens and quotas during import so accounts write to disk with 100% full quota and tier data; switched `list_accounts` read locks to non-blocking `try_read()` to prevent the backend server from stalling frontend instant rendering during cold starts. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)

            -   **Anti-Throttling Jitter Architecture & Strict Idempotency**: Injected 30ms ~ 90ms dynamic hash jitter and integrated 429 rate limit automatic 1.5s backoff retries; enforced lowercase email and Refresh Token deduplication with in-place upserts and orphan file self-healing. (Fixes #3726, Thanks to @jeikl)

(Fixes #3728, Thanks to @bkspi, @lbjlaq)

            -   **Native Command Line Tooling Suite (`at` / `antigravity-tools`)**: Rolled out a lightweight terminal account switcher supporting `at list`, `at current`, and `at switch [query]`, with `--continue` for seamless session recovery. (Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)
        -   **[Proxy Pool Leak Prevention, Multi-Protocol Auth Enhancements & Local Agent Probe Compatibility] (Fixes #3703, Fixes #3729, Fixes #3730, PR #3708, PR #3706, Thanks to @CarlitoDon, @cubelikeplayDaniel, @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)**:
            -   **Bound Account Anti-Direct Leak Safeguard**: Refactored dedicated proxy routing so that if a bound proxy is unhealthy and no public pool node exists, the request strictly continues over the bound proxy (preferring network errors over silent direct connections), eradicating source IP leaks to Google authorization systems. (Fixes #3703, PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)

            -   **Web/Headless Mode In-Memory Hot Reloading**: Fully synchronized `only_raw_quota_models` write locks and client cache invalidations for 100% feature parity with GUI desktop environments. (Fixes #3730, Thanks to @Ran-Xing, @lbjlaq)

(Ref #3720, Thanks to @jeikl)

            -   **Native & Function Tool Topological Ordering**: Preserved relative declaration priority between custom function tools and native tools (GoogleSearch, CodeExecution). (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Google countTokens Dedicated Envelope Cleaning**: Introduced `align_count_tokens_envelope` to strip extraneous `project`, `model`, `userAgent`, and `safetySettings`, eliminating top-level envelope pollution 400 errors. (Ref #3697, PR #3702, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **PipelineTokenEstimator Tool Thought Signature Accounting**: Extracted implicit thought signatures in Claude `tool_use` and `server_tool_use` blocks during token estimation, preventing deep tool sessions from undercounting and breaching 200k auto-compact thresholds. (Ref #3697, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Session Fingerprint Multimodal Stabilization & Responses Concurrency Guards**: Stabilized single-character short input fingerprints and summarized image metadata features to prevent hash avalanches; managed completions concurrency with RAII Drop Guards. (Ref #3697, PR #3699, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(Ref #425, Ref #146, PR #3746, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(Fixes #3744, Ref #3748)
            -   **Adaptive Regular Policy Restoration on Mini Dismissal & Refocus**: Encapsulated `hide_mini_window` to safely restore `ActivationPolicy::Regular` upon blur, tray click, or close when the main console remains alive (`is_visible() || is_minimized()`), while maintaining `Regular` dynamically when the main window regains focus. (Ref #3748)

(Ref #3748)

Phase 1 captures a read-only snapshot `Vec<(String, usize)>` of expired jailed accounts via `states.iter()` and cleanly terminates iteration to release shard read locks; Phase 2 safely transitions each account to `Probing` state using write locks, completely eradicating DashMap shard self-deadlocks that caused port 8045 to freeze and become unresponsive. (Fixes #3749, PR #3751, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

            -   **In-Memory Pool Sanitization Defense**: In `token_manager.rs` (`load_single_account`, `get_available_models_from_json`, and live quota refresh), thoroughly stripped all Argon variants from `model_quotas`, `available_models`, and `model_limits` when an account lacks dedicated Argon group authorization, preventing unauthorized accounts from usurping quota and causing 503 circuit-breaker trips.             -   **Adaptive Retry Boundary Alignment**: Guaranteed in `common.rs` that fast account rotation (50ms) for hard quota depletion applies exclusively to round 1, transitioning smoothly into standard backoff on subsequent rounds. (Fixes #3745)

(Ref #3733, Ref #3738, Ref #3741)

(Ref #3738)

(Ref #3733, Ref #3738)

(Fixes #3710)

(Fixes #3710, Ref #3723)

            -   **TTFT Header Watchdog & Total Budget Convergence**: Tightened primary streaming response header watchdog to a 30s base (elastically extended up to 45s for large payloads) with a 25s fallback margin, bounding total fallback budget to 55~70s (30s for non-streaming) and eliminating 85s~140s hangs on congested endpoints. (Fixes #3713, PR #3714, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

(PR #3714)

(PR #3714)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.10 (2026-10-23)**:
        -   **[Cross-Model Signature Architecture Refactor: Aligning with Official Antigravity Sentinel Discipline & Eliminating Claude Concurrent Tool 400 Errors]**:
            -   **Official Base64 Sentinel Parity**: Reverse-engineered official Antigravity client traces and Proto3 JSON conventions, standardizing the global sentinel constant from plaintext `skip_thought_signature_validator` to the official 44-byte Base64 ciphertext `c2tpcF90aG91Z2h0X3NpZ25hdHVyZV92YWxpZGF0b3I=`; retained backward-compatible recognition for legacy plaintext sentinels (`LEGACY_SENTINEL_SIGNATURE`) to guarantee zero regression on cached turns.
            -   **Claude Zero-Sentinel & Concurrent Tool Cleanliness**: Fully exempted Claude models from synthetic sentinel injection to satisfy Anthropic's cryptographic verification engine; in concurrent tool use scenarios, signatures exclusively anchor on the first non-thought part (first tool or prose), while all subsequent concurrent tools strictly omit `thoughtSignature`, permanently eliminating `Invalid signature in thinking block` 400 rejections.
            -   **Claude ➔ Gemini Transition Foreign Tool Passports**: When switching from Claude back to Gemini, the inbound Gatekeeper automatically detects foreign Claude tools carrying the `toolu_` prefix and equips every foreign `functionCall` (including subsequent concurrent tools) with a Base64 sentinel passport for seamless Gemini engine verification bypass, while strictly keeping prose parts pure without sentinels.
            -   **Gemini ➔ Claude Transition History Sanitization**: When switching to Claude, the inbound pipeline completely purges legacy Gemini short signatures and sentinels, ensuring historical tools and prose dispatch cleanly without contaminating Claude verification.
            -   **Zero-Database-Persistence Protection**: Upheld the architectural principle of "in-memory on-demand sentinel injection", enforcing strict guards in `normalize_and_heal_signature` so neither plaintext nor Base64 sentinels ever persist into SQLite `tool_signatures` or `thinking_records`, eliminating long-term cache pollution.
            -   **Added Cross-Model Transition Assertions**: Added `test_gatekeeper_foreign_claude_tools_switched_to_gemini_get_base64_sentinel` integration unit tests to safeguard against future regression.

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.9 (2026-10-23)**:
        -   **[Eradicating Linux Window Focus Hijacking & Separating CLI Token Sync] (Fixes #3728, Thanks to @bkspi)**:
            -   **Completely Prohibited Dynamic Process Execution for Version Probing**: Removed the fallback invocation of `Command::new(exe_path).arg("--version")` on Linux. For Electron desktop applications that do not cleanly terminate on `--version`, running the binary triggered single-instance IPC handlers that forcibly restored and focused the existing window every few seconds (or auto-started the app when closed). Replaced with strictly safe static metadata inspection. (Fixes #3728, Thanks to @bkspi)
            -   **Symlink Resolution & In-Memory asar Parsing**: Automatically resolves symlinks via `fs::canonicalize` to locate the true installation root across standard Linux paths (`/opt/Antigravity/`, `/usr/share/`, etc.) for `resources/app/package.json` and `product.json`. Implemented a lightweight in-memory `extract_version_from_asar_bytes` parser for asar-bundled assets, safely falling back to defaults on failure without ever spawning external processes. (Fixes #3728)
            -   **60-Second In-Memory Version Caching**: Introduced a global in-memory cache with a 60-second TTL (`VERSION_CACHE`) in `get_antigravity_version_with_path`, completely eliminating disk I/O churn caused by frequent frontend polling and window focus events. (Fixes #3728)
            -   **Decoupled Classic App Switches from Overwriting CLI Credentials**: Added an explicit `sync_agy_cli` flag to `write_to_file_credentials`. Switching the Classic desktop edition now strictly updates the system keyring and generic credentials without clobbering `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token`, ensuring CLI credentials remain isolated unless explicitly switching the CLI client. (Fixes #3728)
            -   **Target-Isolated Client Status Fallback**: Hardened `detect_active_clients_status` so that if a client cannot be directly matched against the system keyring, it falls back to its own tracked target ID (`current_classic_account_id` / `current_agy_account_id` / `current_ide_account_id`) instead of returning `None`, preventing frontend UI fallbacks from displaying the same global account across multiple clients. (Fixes #3728)
        -   **[Full Compatibility for Gemini Protocol URL Query (?key=) Authentication] (Fixes #3729, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)**:
            -   **Supported Standard URL Query API Key Extraction**: Resolved an issue where Google Gemini official standard REST requests and third-party clients (e.g. Cherry Studio) passing credentials via URL query parameters (e.g. `?key=YOUR_API_KEY` or `?alt=sse&key=...`) were erroneously blocked with `401 Unauthorized` due to header-only validation. Refactored the `extract_api_key` credential extraction pipeline to seamlessly support `Authorization: Bearer`, `x-api-key`, `x-goog-api-key`, and URL query `?key=`. (Fixes #3729, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
            -   **Unified User Token Extraction in Disabled Auth Mode**: Extended the universal credential extraction pipeline to `auth_mode = Off`, ensuring User Token identities and usage metrics are accurately extracted and recorded regardless of whether credentials are provided via HTTP headers or Gemini standard URL queries. (Fixes #3729)
        -   **[Complete In-Memory Hot-Reload for "Only Expose Raw Quota Models" in Web/Headless Mode] (Fixes #3730, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)**:
            -   **Restored In-Memory Hot-Reload for Web Config Save**: Fixed an issue in Web / Headless (VPS) environments where saving settings via `admin_save_config` omitted acquiring the write lock for `only_raw_quota_models`, preventing changes to "Only expose raw quota models" from taking immediate effect on `/v1/models` without a server restart. (Fixes #3730, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
            -   **Full Alignment with Desktop Hot-Reload Pipelines**: Parity-aligned Web mode with Desktop by integrating hot-reload updates for `debug_logging`, `upstream.set_user_agent_override`, `monitor.set_capture_health_logs`, `hub_version` fingerprinting, and upstream proxy / proxy pool client cache invalidation. (Fixes #3730)

(Ref #3726)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.7 (2026-10-22)**:
        -   **[Instant Cold-Start Account Rendering & Full Quota Persistence Fix] (Fixes #3726)**:

After import completion, subsequent cold starts load locally without delayed online backfilling, completely eliminating incremental loading from 0 to N. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Decoupled Cold-Start Startup Locks (Zero-Latency UI Rendering)**: Refactored `commands::list_accounts` to acquire the TokenManager runtime reference via non-blocking `try_read()`, ensuring frontend account list queries are never stalled while the background admin server initializes under an exclusive write lock. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Prioritized Direct Modern Index Deserialization**: Streamlined `load_account_index_in_dir` to directly deserialize the modern `AccountIndex` first (~0.5ms), bypassing redundant legacy format probes and file I/O operations during normal restarts. (Fixes #3726)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.6 (2026-10-22)**:
        -   **[Asynchronous High-Performance Batch Import & Index Persistence Overhaul] (Fixes #3726)**:
            -   **Asynchronous Streaming Concurrency & Backpressure**: Introduced the `batch_import_accounts` high-performance pipeline backed by `buffer_unordered(6)` to strictly bound in-flight active tasks, slashing the import duration for 494 accounts from 28 minutes to ~15-20 seconds (>60x speedup) and eradicating memory spikes and kernel OOM terminations caused by unbounded `join_all`. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Scientific Anti-Throttling & IP Protection**: Implemented 30ms ~ 90ms dynamic hash jitter to desynchronize concurrent request dispatches, preventing simultaneous requests from hitting Google endpoints on the same millisecond; built-in 1.5s automatic backoff retry on HTTP 429 / rate limits paired with two-stage lightweight validation (verifying Token and user info during import while deferring heavy quota queries), eliminating Google WAF ban risks. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Eliminating Small-File Iteration Traps & DOM Re-render Avalanches**: Overhauled `detect_active_clients_status` to bypass iterating hundreds of individual JSON files and SQLite queries via direct in-memory index comparisons, throttling frontend polling from 5s intervals to window focus-driven triggers; collapsed batch refresh and import index persistence from N writes to a single atomic commit; restricted import UI updates to a lightweight progress bar to eliminate 494 full DOM re-renders. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Strict Idempotency & Orphan File Self-Healing**: Enforced dual deduplication via normalized lowercase emails and Refresh Tokens with in-place upserts, reusing existing account IDs and overwriting existing files regardless of repeated additions or delete-readd cycles; introduced `clean_duplicate_account_files` to detect and safely clean up orphan files on disk, ensuring exactly one physical file per account. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Legacy Single-File Auto-Migration & Resilient `-backup` Protection**: Automatically detects legacy single-file stores (e.g. legacy monolithic `accounts.json`), safely generates modern segmented `accounts/{uuid}.json` files, and creates `accounts.json-backup` or `<file>-backup` files with automatic recovery fallbacks if index corruption occurs. (Fixes #3726)
            -   **Unified Batch Import/Export Across All Environments**: Fully unified high-performance batch import and export workflows across Desktop (Tauri event progress modal), Web (Axum `/accounts/batch-import` endpoint), and CLI (`at import <file>` / `at export` commands). (Fixes #3726)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.5 (2026-10-22)**:
        -   **[Adaptive QoS Scheduling System with Pure Peer Calibration & Ladder Probing] (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)**:
            -   **Pure TTFT Transient Extraction**: Leverages the pure elapsed duration from request dispatch to upstream first-chunk arrival as the core physical metric, completely stripping output length and streaming transfer noise to detect silent QoS tiering demotion. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)
            -   **5-Stage Pure Peer Baseline & Anti-False-Positive Shield**: Peer baselines strictly exclude self, heterogeneous subscription tiers, disabled accounts, 429 rate-limited/zero-quota accounts, Jailed, Probing, Suspicious, and Half-Open accounts to completely eliminate baseline poisoning; introduces 35s dynamic threshold compensation for deep reasoning models to prevent misjudging thinking latency; applies global outage brakes (pausing penalties when pure peer average exceeds 20s) and an 8s~20s hysteresis deadband to eradicate state boundary jitter. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)
            -   **Transient Tagging & Immediate Session Handover**: Automatically tags an account as `Suspicious` on its first strike (instantly excluding it from peer average calculation while activating drain mode to pause new session allocation); on 2 strikes within a 5-minute sliding window, immediately isolates the account into jail cooldown and forcibly unbinds its active sticky session; the P2C scheduler dynamically selects a pure healthy account and re-welds the sticky session, restoring 100% Prompt Cache efficiency. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)
            -   **Background Ladder Probing & Half-Open Anti-Flapping**: Implements an escalating cooldown schedule (2m -> 5m -> 10m -> 15m -> 20m -> 30m) with cooldown timers strictly reset to 0 upon entering each new tier; background daemon issues lightweight synthetic "Hello" probes (<5 tokens) with an 8-second hard timeout abort; passing probes transition the account to `Half-Open` probation, requiring 2 consecutive real requests under 8s to achieve full health; 30-minute penalty retention directly inherits elevated tiers upon relapse. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)
            -   **Cross-UI Status Transparency & 12-Language i18n**: Dynamically displays yellow status badges (`suspicious`, `jailed`, `probing`, `half_open`) across Account Cards, Tables, Rows, and ProxyMonitor traffic logs with multilingual tooltip hints, automatically disappearing upon full recovery; fully backed by pure monolingual entries across all 12 supported language catalogs. (Fixes #3720, PR #3725)
        -   **[CI Preview Release Asset Cap Protection]**:
            -   **Automated Stale Preview Asset Cleanup**: Configured `removeArtifacts: true` in the GitHub Actions `Sync Preview Channel Release` step, resolving an issue where accumulated pre-release assets reached GitHub's 1000-asset limit per release and caused publishing failures; ensures the preview release mirror only retains the latest version assets and `updater.json`.

Strictly guarded against Windows `NOTIFYICONDATAW.szTip` 128-character buffer limits with safe email truncation for zero overflow. (Fixes #3719, Thanks to @Wiojelt)

            -   **Future-Proof Two-Stage Model Matcher (Pipeline First)**: Overhauled tray quota extraction by eliminating brittle hardcoded model names, prioritizing flagship models while introducing wildcard fallbacks (`gemini-*-pro*`, `gemini-*-flash*`, `*image*`, `*claude*/*sonnet*`) to seamlessly support future upstream model releases. (Fixes #3719)
            -   **Instant Mini View Tray Shortcut & Bidirectional Event Bus**: Added a direct "Mini View" action to the tray context menu backed by `tray://show-mini-view` and `tray://show-window` events, enabling users to instantly pop open or switch to the floating desktop HUD from the tray and smoothly restore the full window upon demand; fully localized across all 12 supported languages. (Fixes #3719)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.3 (2026-10-21)**:
        -   **[Multi-Client Identity Normalization & Anti-Throttling Prompt Defense] (Ref #3720: Antigravity Rate Limiting Investigation)**:
            -   **Broad Identity Normalization for Mainstream AI Agents and CLIs**: Driven by empirical findings from production rate-limiting and QoS demotion investigations (see Issue #3720 for full telemetry and benchmarks), addressed upstream Google's heightened semantic rate-limiting against third-party harnesses (OpenCode, OpenClaw, Grok Build, Cursor, Cascade, Cline, Aider, KiloCode, DSH, Devin, etc.), which previously demoted high-throughput accounts into degraded best-effort queues (causing TTFT to spike from ~4s to 40s–80s+ under compute pressure). Comprehensively upgraded the `PromptSanitizer` engine to detect nominal preambles, host-environment declarations (`running inside / within`, `integrated into`), and natural-language Chinese patterns, harmonizing them into the official Antigravity native identity to strip external wrapper fingerprints and restore official IDE-tier fast-track QoS treatment. (Ref #3720)
            -   **Adaptive Leading Window with Plugin Metadata Penetration**: Extended the system prompt header scan region from a rigid 4-sentence cap to an elastic dual window of up to 8 sentences or 1500 characters, reliably penetrating leading metadata banners (such as OpenClaw's `--- OpenClaw plugin-injected context ---`) to prevent real identity declarations from being pushed out of the scanning window. (Ref #3720)
            -   **Decimal Version Boundary Lookahead & Core Prompt Immunity**: Refactored attribution prefix regexes with lookahead matching for version decimals (e.g. `Grok 4.6`, `GPT-5.2`), preventing decimals from being mistakenly treated as sentence-ending periods. Established an ingress whitelist bypass ensuring core framework prompts (`JeikCode`), XML delimiters, and neutral developer prompts remain 100% unmutated. (Ref #3720)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.2 (2026-10-20)**:
        -   **[Hermes Agent / Local Probe Endpoint Compatibility & OpenAI Model Context Metadata] (PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)**:
            -   **llama.cpp and vLLM Standard Probe Endpoints Integration**: Introduced `/props`, `/v1/props` (returning `n_ctx`, `n_predict`, with optional `?model=` query support) and `/version`, `/v1/version` (returning app and version identifiers) endpoints, completely resolving probe 404 errors and context detection fallback warnings for Hermes Agent, llama.cpp toolchains, and vLLM clients. (PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)
            -   **OpenAI Model List & Retrieval Context Metadata Enrichment**: Injected `context_length`, `max_context_length`, `max_input_tokens`, `max_model_len`, `max_tokens`, and `max_completion_tokens` into `/v1/models` and `/v1/models/{model}` responses, prioritizing `OfficialModelCatalog` limits with graceful fallback by model family. Added catalog fallback in model retrieval for direct official model lookups. (PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)
            -   **Probe Logging Silence and Health Check Exemption Alignment**: Added probe endpoints to the `is_health_check` bypass list in auth middleware, demoting probe access logs to `trace` heartbeat level to prevent traffic log noise and granting unauthenticated probe handshake in `AllExceptHealth` mode. (PR #3708, Thanks to @CarlitoDon)
        -   **[Model Warmup & Connectivity Probe Model Route Alignment] (Fixes #3715)**:
            -   **Internal Warmup (`/internal/warmup`) Model Route Normalization**: Resolved an issue where the warmup endpoint forwarded derived bare model IDs (such as `gemini-3.8-flash` or `gpt-oss-120b`) directly to Google upstream as raw entity names, causing Google to return 404 (`Requested entity was not found.`); integrated `resolve_model_route_with_effort` at the warmup entry point to align bare models with canonical physical models (e.g. `gemini-3.8-flash-tiered`), eliminating false-positive 404 test failures and synchronizing traffic monitor logging. (Fixes #3715)
        -   **[Gemini 503 Overload In-Place Self-Healing & Prefix Cache Protection] (Fixes #3713)**:
            -   **Thinking Budget In-Place Degradation Self-Healing (Pipeline First)**: Resolved an issue where unbounded adaptive thinking budgets (`thinkingBudget: -1` or > 4000) triggered Google upstream 503 (`The model is overloaded.`) under peak compute pressure, which formerly caused unnecessary multi-account failover loops and wiped out upstream Prefix Cache. Introduced `try_degrade_thinking_budget` and `is_server_overloaded` in the unified pipeline to safely clamp thinking budget to 4000 (Medium tier) on the bound account and retry in-place once, recovering in $\sim 95\%$ of overload situations while maintaining 100% prefix cache continuity before falling back to cross-account rotation. (Fixes #3713)
        -   **[OpenAI Compatible Endpoint Streaming finish_reason Guard & Model Ending Defense] (Fixes #3716)**:
            -   **OpenAI Stream Transformer Terminal finish_reason Completion**: Resolved an issue where Gemini upstream closing without an explicit `finishReason` (or sending only `usageMetadata` frames) caused the stream handler to omit the terminating choice chunk, leading downstream clients (such as OpenCode and Cline) to mark responses as `finish=unknown`; added a terminal state machine fallback in `emit_finalize()` to guarantee emitting a chunk with valid `finish_reason` ("stop" or "tool_calls") and usage metadata prior to emitting `[DONE]`. (Fixes #3716)
            -   **Model-Ending Defense Granular Sanitization (Pipeline First)**: Refactored transit defense node `ensure_gemini_payload_ends_with_user`; strictly distinguishing tool responses from text turns against Google's upstream restriction on model endings. Tool response turns containing `functionResponse` are strictly preserved untouched to safeguard agent state machines (Fixes #3629), empty trailing assistant turns from interrupted client retries are automatically popped to restore valid user endings, and non-empty model turns receive a compliant user primer (`"."`), eliminating Google 400 (`Requests ending with a model turn are not supported.`) errors and 40+ retry storms. (Fixes #3716)
        -   **[Antigravity CLI Native Credential Switcher & Seamless Session Continuation] (Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)**:
            -   **Native CLI Tool Suite (`at` / `antigravity-tools`)**: Introduced a dedicated lightweight CLI switcher supporting account listing (`at list`), active client state inspection across IDE / Keychain (`at current`), and smart account switching (`at switch [query]`) by 1-based index, email, alias, or automatic round-robin; supports `-c` / `--continue` to immediately resume existing `agy` sessions upon Keychain credential rotation. (Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)
            -   **HTTP Management API Smart Matching**: Enhanced `/api/accounts/switch` to accept emails, aliases, and indices alongside UUIDs, automatically resolving to canonical account IDs and coordinating `targetIde: "agy"` Keychain writes. (Fixes #3686)
            -   **Full-Fledged Native OAuth & 429 Session Resume Loop**: Upgraded CLI sync wrapper templates by removing intrusive API Key / Base URL exports, preserving official OAuth Thinking Budget and `/plugin` commands while wrapping `agy_cli` in an automatic round-robin retry and `--continue` resumption loop upon 429 quota exhaustion. (Fixes #3686, Thanks to @Ran-Xing)

    *   **v4.9.9-beta.1 (2026-10-18)**:
        -   **[Proxy Pool Anti-Leak Hardening, URL Credential Sanitization & Resilient Health Checks] (Fixes #3703, Ref #3323, PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Bound Account Direct IP Leak Prevention**: Overhauled bound proxy routing decisions. When an account's designated proxy is flagged as unhealthy, the gateway prioritizes auto-failover to available healthy nodes in the public pool; if no public proxy is available, requests are strictly routed through the bound proxy regardless of its health state (preferring upstream network failure over direct outbound connections), completely eliminating server VPS IP leaks to Google authorization systems and preventing risk-control account bans. (Fixes #3703, PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **WHATWG URL Credential Strip Order & Encrypted Ciphertext Protection**: Aligned with the WHATWG URL standard by clearing password before username, ensuring clean credential extraction across HTTP, HTTPS, and SOCKS5h proxies. Furthermore, if cross-machine migration leaves an undecrypted `ag_enc_` password without embedded fallback credentials, the gateway refuses to forward encrypted ciphertext over plaintext HTTP CONNECT headers. (PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Relaxed Health Check Handshake Timeout**: Increased TCP connect timeout from 5s to 10s and total probe timeout to 15s to prevent false-negative health check failures on high-latency or cross-border multi-hop proxy chains during TLS tunnel negotiation. (PR #3706, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Gemini Protocol Strict Alternation, Tool Ordering & SQLite Thinking Decompression] (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Strict Role Alternation Enforcement (user <-> model)**: Introduced a state machine during auto-heal continuation and media splitting to seamlessly merge continuation prompts into the preceding user turn's parts whenever the previous turn was user-authored, preventing consecutive `[..., user, user]` sequences from triggering Google HTTP 400 rejection (`Please ensure that multiturn requests alternate between user and model roles`). (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Native & Function Tool Topological Ordering**: Fixed tool declaration reordering that erroneously deferred function declarations behind native tools, strictly preserving the relative priority of client-defined tools alongside GoogleSearch and CodeExecution. (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Model-Aware Rate Limit Inspection**: Added target-model-specific quota exhaustion checks in `has_available_account` to guarantee instant failover to external provider models when account quota runs out. (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Stream Lifecycle Double-Finalization Guard**: Added terminal state inspection after flushing trailing stream buffers, preventing duplicate `[DONE]` frames or redundant `message_stop` events from breaking client stream decoders. (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **SQLite GZIP BLOB In-Memory Unpack for Thinking Signatures**: Replaced ineffective SQL `LIKE` queries against binary compressed BLOBs (for thoughts $\ge 128$ bytes) with in-memory GZIP decompression, restoring signature retrieval by thought snippet. (Fixes #3695, PR #3696, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[countTokens Envelope Purification, Global Thinking Effort Inheritance & Token Estimation Signatures] (Ref #3697, PR #3702, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Google countTokens Dedicated Envelope Alignment**: Introduced `align_count_tokens_envelope` to strip extraneous top-level keys (`project`, `model`, `userAgent`, `requestId`) and inner `safetySettings` before forwarding to Google v1internal `:countTokens`, preventing upstream 400 Bad Request errors. (Ref #3697, PR #3702, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Global Gateway Thinking Effort Inheritance**: Fixed `resolve_custom_budget` resolution so that Bare Flash and Tiered models inherit the gateway's globally configured reasoning effort (`tb_config.effort`) when requests lack explicit client effort parameters, preventing unintended unbounded reasoning token expenditure. (Ref #3697, PR #3702, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Tool Use Thought Signature Token Estimation**: Enhanced `PipelineTokenEstimator` for Claude `tool_use` and `server_tool_use` blocks to account for embedded thought signatures (`signature` / `thoughtSignature`), resolving a critical undercount issue where 300k+ token sessions breached the 200k auto-compaction boundary. (Ref #3697, PR #3705, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Adaptive Watchdog Timeout for Terminal Endpoints & Orphan Tool Result Safe Downgrade] (Ref #3697, PR #3701, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Full Watchdog Timeout Coverage on Terminal Endpoints**: Extended `tokio::time::timeout` protection to terminal and single-endpoint upstream requests, eliminating hanging connections when an upstream server stalls on response headers. (Ref #3697, PR #3701, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Orphan Tool Result Identification & Safe Text Downgrade**: Normalized missing tool name fallbacks to `"unknown"` instead of hyphenated `tool_use_id` strings, allowing downstream pipeline Pass 3 self-healing to safely convert orphan tool results into user text blocks without violating Vertex function name regex rules. (Ref #3697, PR #3701, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Session Fingerprint Multimodal Stability, Short Input Preservation & OpenAI Concurrency Guard] (Ref #3697, PR #3699, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Multi-Turn Stability for Short User Inputs (`"1"`, `"y"`)**: Relaxed fingerprint text extraction thresholds, preventing single-character user replies from falling back to full-body hashing and causing session ID drift across turns. (Ref #3697, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Multimodal Input Summary Hashing**: Extracted compact metadata summaries for image and audio inputs, avoiding memory spikes and hash instability caused by serializing multi-megabyte Base64 payloads. (Ref #3697, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Unclosed System Tag Boundary Protection**: Hardened prefix sanitization to preserve user code containing symbols like `[System.IO.File]` and unclosed brackets, preventing premature text truncation. (Ref #3697, PR #3700, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **OpenAI handle_completions In-Flight Guard & Path Alias Normalization**: Added RAII drop guards for streaming and non-streaming completions to eliminate ghost concurrency leaks, while normalizing `/responses` and `/responses/compact` route aliases. (Ref #3697, PR #3699, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

When Antigravity Classic is running and the active account's quota falls below the configured threshold, the engine automatically selects the healthiest available account from the pool based on threshold satisfaction, tier priority (Pro/Ultra > Free), highest remaining quota, and LRU anti-oscillation. Seamlessly restarts `language_server` via supervisor within 2 seconds while preserving Keyring credentials, guarded by a 120-second cooldown lock and desktop notifications. (Ref #3509)
            -   **Streamlined Dual Quota Pool Monitoring & Threshold Control**: Eliminated cluttered per-model selection tags in favor of intuitive dual-pool cards aligned with the dashboard — "Gemini Pool (Pro / Flash / Image)" and "Third-Party Models (Claude 3.7 / 3.5 / GPT)", with customizable trigger thresholds from 0% to 99%.
            -   **Removal of Redundant Legacy Account Sync & UI Streamlining**: Removed the confusing legacy "Auto Sync Current Account" card from Account Settings and removed its unused background interval timer, keeping the settings interface clean and focused.
            -   **Full 12-Language Localization**: Synchronized complete translations across all 12 supported languages including Chinese (Simplified & Traditional), English, Korean, Japanese, Russian, Spanish, Portuguese, Turkish, Vietnamese, Arabic, and Malay.
        -   **[Antigravity CLI (agy) Native Evolution, Anti-Telemetry Tracking & Dynamic Model Selection] (Ref #3686, Ref #3630)**:
            -   **Smooth Migration to Antigravity CLI (agy)**: Officially migrated the CLI synchronization module to support Antigravity CLI (`agy`), while maintaining 100% backward compatibility for legacy `gemini` CLI detection and restoration. (Ref #3686)
            -   **Frontend Dynamic Model Selection & Real-Time Reflection**: Rebuilt CLI model onboarding with a dropdown interface supporting custom model selection; the backend reflects the live configured model in real-time upon state inspection.
            -   **Automatic Shell Wrapper Management & Thinking Effort Disassembly**: Automatically identifies and wraps `~/.local/bin/agy`, injecting `GEMINI_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL`, and anti-telemetry headers; intelligently disassembles base model names and reasoning effort tiers (e.g. `gemini-3.8-flash-low` into `--model gemini-3.8-flash --effort low`).
            -   **Independent Active Account Tracking & Multi-Client Badges**: Added `detect_active_clients_status` to independently identify live active accounts across Antigravity Classic, Antigravity IDE (`state.vscdb`), and Antigravity CLI (`agy` credentials & Keyring), displaying dedicated contextual badges (`[Classic]`, `[IDE]`, `[CLI]`) and row highlighting. (Ref #3630, Ref #3686)
