            -   **Full 403 Body Capture & Action URL Extraction (validation_url / appeal_url)**: Eliminated premature discarding of 403 HTTP response bodies in `modules/quota.rs`. Full Google RPC error payload details are now parsed to extract `validation_url` (human/captcha verification links) and `appeal_url` (Terms of Service appeal links) into the account model, lighting up actionable frontend buttons.
            -   **Decouple Temporary Verification (VALIDATION_REQUIRED) from Permanent Ban**: Refactored gateway handlers across Gemini, Claude, and OpenAI to make temporary validation and permanent ban (`set_forbidden`) mutually exclusive, avoiding accidental permanent disabling during temporary observation windows.
            -   **Automatic Deadlock Clearing & Closed-Loop Recovery on Successful Probe**: When an account completes verification externally and quota probing succeeds, the system automatically clears the `proxy_disabled` flag and resets stale 403 error reasons, reloading the account back into the pool.
        -   **[Align with Claude Code 2.1.301 Protocol Specification, Full-Link Traceability & Claude 5 Models] (PR #3689, Ref #3684, Ref #3686, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Anti-WAF Identity Sanitization & Agent SDK CLI Preamble Alignment**: Extended `RE_WAF_TRIGGER_HEADERS` and prompt normalization in `prompt_sanitizer.rs` to strip Agent SDK CLI preambles and clean new telemetry headers (`cc_workload`, `cc_is_subagent`), preventing Google Cloud Code WAF 429 rate limit errors. (PR #3689, Ref #3684, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Full-Link Downstream Traceability (request-id)**: Unified injection of `request-id: req_{trace_id}` across streaming, aggregated JSON, and non-streaming responses in `handlers/claude.rs`. (PR #3689, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Claude Protocol Schema Alignment & Adaptive Thinking Display**: Added `ThinkingConfig.display` and `Usage.cache_creation` to cleanly receive and relay cache creation token distribution metrics. (PR #3689, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Claude 5 Model Family Registration & CLI Sync**: Registered 8 core Claude 5 model definitions in `cli_sync.rs` (with 256k context window). (PR #3689, Ref #3686, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Long-Context First Token Latency Stabilization, 429 Anti-DDoS Lockout Capping & Multi-Protocol Signature Hardening] (Fixes #3697, Ref #3693, Ref #3506, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Quota Reset Delay vs. Anti-DDoS Header Maximum Arbitration**: Added extraction for natural language duration patterns (e.g. `Individual quota reached. Resets in 1h19m6s.`) and structured `quotaResetDelay`; arbitrates `Retry-After` headers and body delays via `max(header_delay, body_delay)`, completely preventing Google front-door anti-DDoS 8-second headers from prematurely unlocking exhausted accounts and causing 429 loops. (Fixes #3697, Ref #3506)
            -   **Reactive In-Memory Quota Depletion**: Unconditionally marks model quotas to 0% in-memory upon encountering `QuotaExhausted` errors, preventing affinity routing and token discovery from selecting exhausted accounts before asynchronous background polling.
            -   **Thinking Store Phase 3 Matching 75% Speedup & Decoupled Watermark Eviction**: Precomputes thought normalization outside nested turn loops, eliminating redundant allocations during multi-turn history hydration; decouples session watermark TTL cleanup from session count limits to prevent unbounded memory growth under low-activity sessions.
            -   **Stream Collector Thinking Merge & Dynamic Message Turn Cache Indexing**: Merges adjacent streaming thinking chunks into a single unified `thought: true` part during SSE aggregation; dynamically indexes thought signatures by active interaction turn count rather than hardcoded 1.
        -   **[Prevent OOM Crashes on Cyclic JSON Schema Expansion & Reliability Hardening] (PR #3694, Fixes #3690, Ref #3682, Ref #3693, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Cyclic Self-Reference Termination & Safe Degradation**: Introduced active traversal tracking `active_refs` during recursive reference flattening for branching schemas ($b \ge 2$), degrading safely into terminal empty objects annotated with `"(Cyclic $ref: ...)"` and preventing $2^{64}$ exponential memory explosions (SIGKILL 137). (PR #3694, Fixes #3690, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Schema Array Normalization & Terminal ARRAY Items Guard**: Strips illegal `properties` on array nodes while ensuring compliant `items`, preventing Gemini Protobuf 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT errors. (PR #3694, Ref #3682, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Adaptive Streaming Peek Timeout for Reasoning Models**: Implemented `calculate_adaptive_peek_timeout_full` to extend first-chunk pre-read timeout baseline from 30s to 60s/90s/120s for thinking/reasoning models, preventing premature cancellations and rotation storms. (PR #3694, Ref #3693, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Zero-Downtime Atomic Account Pool Reload**: Redesigned reload into local batch loading followed by atomic `retain` and `insert`, eliminating transient `503 Token pool is empty` errors during reloads. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Anti-Regression Rate Limit Protection & Order-Agnostic ErrorInfo Scanning**: Preserves longer unexpired quota backoff periods against shorter subsequent generic 429 delays, and scans all entries in RPC `details` arrays. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Authorization Header Sensitive Masking & Single-Pass Regex Code Restoration**: Marked `Authorization` headers as `sensitive` in logs and upgraded code block restoration to single-pass regex replacement. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Eliminate Ineffective Prod Endpoint, Relax Streaming Watchdog to 120s & Preserve Roles] (PR #3691, PR #3672, PR #3687, PR #3685, Fixes #3506, Ref #3525, Ref #3671, Thanks to @EricZhou05, @blackdm666, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Eliminate 100% 429-Yielding Prod Public Endpoint**: Completely removed Prod from `V1_INTERNAL_BASE_URL_FALLBACKS`, retaining only official native primary `Daily` and verified backup `Sandbox` endpoints. (PR #3691, Ref #3525, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
            -   **Recalibrate Streaming Watchdog First-Chunk Timeout to 120s Baseline**: Relaxed streaming first-chunk timeout to 120s baseline (`max(120, body_len / 64KB + 120)`), fully accommodating model reasoning and cold-cache preheating. (PR #3691, Fixes #3506, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
            -   **Preserve System/User/Assistant Roles in Array Inputs**: Reused `convert_codex_to_openai_request` to accurately restore structured message roles per turn in Responses-style requests. (PR #3672, Fixes #3671, Thanks to @blackdm666)
            -   **Eliminate 0ms Optimistic Reset Storms**: Replaced optimistic reset with physical asynchronous cooling (<= 5s) and explicit cooldown returns (> 5s); introduced two-round uniform rotation (150ms delay) and linear backoff, lowering 429 error rates to 0.0%. (PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Case-Insensitive Retry-After Header Parsing**: Supported case-insensitive matching for `"Wait "` and `"wait "` to ensure proper `Retry-After: N` header generation on 503 responses. (PR #3687, Fixes #3506, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
        -   **[Traffic Monitor Inspector Overhaul: Single-Tab Postman Architecture, Structured Payloads & Dual-Link HAR Export]**:
            -   **Unified Postman-Style Single-Tab Layout**: Restructured payload details into Request, Forwarded, and Response tabs with compact metadata and non-wrapping token consumption status.
            -   **Dual View Modes**: Supports toggling between Structured View (system prompts, dialogue bubbles, tool declarations, thinking blocks) and Raw Code View.
            -   **Key-Value Headers Table & Masking**: Two-column key-value tables with instant copying and `Bearer sk-***` masking toggles.
            -   **Standard Dual-Link HAR 1.2 Export**: Exports client-to-gateway and gateway-to-upstream entries with 100% complete 3-message capture and one-click cURL/JSON copy.


    *   **v4.9.8-beta.10 (2026-10-17)**:
        -   **[Prevent OOM Crashes on Cyclic JSON Schema Expansion & Array Normalization] (PR #3694, Fixes #3690, Ref #3682, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Cyclic Self-Reference Termination & Safe Degradation**: Introduced active traversal tracking `active_refs` during recursive reference flattening for branching schemas ($b \ge 2$, e.g. recursive binary trees `left: $ref, right: $ref`). Upon detecting self-references within the active chain, expansion degrades safely into a terminal empty object annotated with `"(Cyclic $ref: ...)"`, completely preventing $2^{64}$ exponential subtree replication and eliminating Linux VPS/server OOM crashes (SIGKILL 137). (PR #3694, Fixes #3690, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Schema Array Normalization & Terminal ARRAY Items Guard**: Refined tool schema normalization for array nodes that erroneously include `properties`. Strips illegal `properties` while guaranteeing compliant `items`; enforces a terminal guard ensuring all finalized ARRAY schemas carry valid `items`, preventing Gemini Protobuf 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT rejection on union array types. (PR #3694, Ref #3682, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Adaptive Streaming Peek Timeout for Reasoning Models & TTFT Protection] (PR #3694, Ref #3693, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Extended Peek Grace Period for Reasoning Models**: Implemented `calculate_adaptive_peek_timeout_full` across Claude and OpenAI endpoints for thinking/reasoning models (`thinkingConfig`, `reasoning_effort`, `o1/o3/o4` series). Extends the first-chunk pre-read (Peek) timeout baseline from 30s to 60s (and dynamically scales up to 90s/120s for large prompt contexts), preventing premature 30s timeout cancellations and pool rotation retry storms caused by deep reasoning TTFT preheating. (PR #3694, Ref #3693, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Zero-Downtime Atomic Account Pool Reload & 503 Elimination] (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Smooth Lossless Account Reload**: Eliminated premature flushing of the global `self.tokens` map during `load_accounts` which previously caused concurrent requests to hit transient `503 Token pool is empty` errors. Redesigned reload into an asynchronous local batch load followed by atomic `retain` (removing deleted accounts) and `insert` (updating active accounts), maintaining 100% pool availability during hot reloads. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Anti-Regression Rate Limit Protection & Order-Agnostic ErrorInfo Scanning] (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Anti-Regression Lock Duration Check**: Added anti-regression validation in `parse_from_error_with_mode`. When an account already holds a longer, unexpired lock (e.g. 300s quota exhaustion backoff), subsequent concurrent generic 429 errors with shorter default delays (60s) are ignored, preserving the longer cooldown window. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Full Details Array Scanning**: Iterates over all entries in Google RPC `details` arrays rather than only index 0, preventing missed rate limit classifications when `google.rpc.ErrorInfo` is preceded by other items like `google.rpc.Help`. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Authorization Header Sensitive Masking & Prompt Single-Pass Code Restoration] (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Header Value Sensitivity Flagging**: Explicitly marked `Authorization` header values sent to upstream Google endpoints as `sensitive`, automatically masking Bearer tokens as `[sensitive]` across all debug and tracing logs. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Single-Pass Non-Recursive Code Restoration**: Replaced iterative string substitution with regex-based atomic single-pass placeholder restoration in `PromptSanitizer`, eliminating 2^N expansion risks if code snippets contain placeholder patterns. Extended identity boundaries with trailing punctuation (`! ? ; ！ ？ ；`) to prevent greedy truncation of subsequent developer instructions. (PR #3694, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.9 (2026-10-17)**:
        -   **[Eliminate Ineffective Prod Endpoint & Extend Streaming First-Chunk Watchdog to 120s] (PR #3691, Fixes #3506, Ref #3525, Thanks to @EricZhou05)**:
            -   **Eliminate 100% 429-Yielding Prod Public Endpoint**: Empirical multi-scale benchmarks demonstrated that the public Prod endpoint (`cloudcode-pa.googleapis.com`) unconditionally returns `429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` within ~1s across all context window sizes, acting as an invalid fallback that triggers false-positive account rate-limiting and 503 error loops. Completely removed Prod from `V1_INTERNAL_BASE_URL_FALLBACKS`, retaining only official native primary `Daily` and verified backup `Sandbox` endpoints to sever the fatal failover loop. (PR #3691, Ref #3525, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
            -   **Recalibrate Streaming Watchdog First-Chunk Timeout to 120s Baseline**: Resolved a flaw where the previous adaptive watchdog formula `max(15, body_len / 256KB + 15)` accounted only for network transmission bandwidth while overlooking long-context and Thinking reasoning time-to-first-token (TTFT ~35s), prematurely aborting requests at 16s. Streaming generation (`streamGenerateContent`) first-chunk timeout is now relaxed to a 120-second baseline (`max(120, body_len / 64KB + 120)`), fully accommodating model reasoning and cold-cache preheating while preventing false-positive 429/503 cutoffs; lightweight non-streaming requests retain the 15-second fast-probing timeout. (PR #3691, Fixes #3506, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
        -   **[Preserve Multi-Turn Message Roles for Responses-Style Chat Completions] (PR #3672, Fixes #3671, Thanks to @blackdm666)**:
            -   **Preserve System/User/Assistant Roles in Array Inputs**: Resolved an issue where `/v1/chat/completions` receiving Responses-style payloads (`instructions` / `input` without `messages`) collapsed array `input` items into a single user text via `to_string()`, destroying role boundaries and causing Gemini to echo conversation history rather than answering the latest turn. Reused `convert_codex_to_openai_request` to accurately restore structured message roles per turn while preserving string input compatibility. (PR #3672, Fixes #3671, Thanks to @blackdm666)
        -   **[Intelligent 403 State Identification, Upstream Action URL Extraction & Closed-Loop Self-Healing] (Ref #3630, Ref #3684)**:
            -   **Full 403 Body Capture & Action URL Extraction (validation_url / appeal_url)**: Eliminated premature discarding of 403 HTTP response bodies in `modules/quota.rs`. Full Google RPC error payload details are now parsed via `extract_action_url` to accurately extract `validation_url` (human/captcha verification links) and `appeal_url` (Terms of Service appeal links) from `details.metadata` and persist them into the account model. Integrated with frontend `AccountErrorDialog` to immediately present actionable buttons ("Verify Now", "Copy Verification URL", "Submit Appeal"), completely replacing vague and opaque 403 error states.
            -   **Decouple Temporary Verification (VALIDATION_REQUIRED) from Permanent Ban**: Refactored gateway handlers across Gemini, Claude, and OpenAI (`handlers/gemini.rs`, `handlers/claude.rs`, `handlers/openai.rs`) and warmup probes to make temporary validation and permanent ban (`set_forbidden`) mutually exclusive. Encountering `VALIDATION_REQUIRED` now places the account into a 10-minute temporary observation window while retaining the verification URL, eliminating accidental permanent `proxy_disabled = true` deadlocks for temporary verification prompts.
            -   **Automatic Deadlock Clearing & Closed-Loop Recovery on Successful Probe**: Redesigned `update_account_quota` and `clear_validation_blocked` so that when an account completes verification externally (e.g. via the official IDE or web browser) and subsequent quota probing succeeds (`is_forbidden == false`), the system automatically clears the `proxy_disabled` flag and resets stale 403 error reasons. Dispatches `trigger_account_reload` and broadcasts updated state to the frontend, restoring recovered accounts back into the active proxy pool seamlessly without manual user intervention.
        -   **[Classic Exclusive Auto Hot-Switch on Depleted Quota, Dual-Pool Monitoring & Legacy Sync Cleanup] (Ref #3509)**:
            -   **Automatic Seamless Hot-Switching for Antigravity Classic (Classic Only, IDE & CLI Not Supported)**: Integrated an auto-switch engine directly into the background quota inspection pipeline (`refresh_all_quotas_internal`). When Antigravity Classic is running and the active account's quota falls below the configured threshold, the engine automatically selects the healthiest available account from the pool based on threshold satisfaction, tier priority (Pro/Ultra > Free), highest remaining quota, and LRU anti-oscillation. Because only Classic Standalone features the built-in supervisor that instantly respawns `language_server` upon termination while inheriting Keyring credentials, this feature is exclusive to Classic Standalone (Antigravity IDE and CLI do not support seamless hot-switching due to official client architecture limitations). Hot-switching completes within 2 seconds without restarting the main window, guarded by a 120-second cooldown lock and desktop notifications. (Ref #3509)
            -   **Streamlined Dual Quota Pool Monitoring & Threshold Control**: Eliminated cluttered per-model selection tags in favor of intuitive dual-pool cards aligned with the dashboard — "Gemini Pool (Pro / Flash / Image)" and "Third-Party Models (Claude 3.7 / 3.5 / GPT)", with customizable trigger thresholds from 0% to 99%.
            -   **Removal of Redundant Legacy Account Sync & UI Streamlining**: Removed the confusing legacy "Auto Sync Current Account" card from Account Settings and removed its unused background interval timer, keeping the settings interface clean and focused.
            -   **Full 12-Language Localization**: Synchronized complete translations across all 12 supported languages including Chinese (Simplified & Traditional), English, Korean, Japanese, Russian, Spanish, Portuguese, Turkish, Vietnamese, Arabic, and Malay.
        -   **[Local Account Scanning Alignment & Legacy Backup Removal]**:
            -   **Streamlined Removal of Legacy V1 Backup & UI Simplification**: Completely eliminated the legacy V1 (`~/.antigravity-agent`) import path and divider in the Add Account dialog, focusing the interface cleanly into "Auto-Scan Local Accounts" and "Import Custom DB" with synchronized multi-language typography and improved spatial efficiency.
            -   **Aggregated Classic & IDE Database Scanning (state.vscdb)**: Introduced `db::get_all_antigravity_db_paths()` to remove the hardcoded restriction that previously limited scanning to Classic `Antigravity`. Now concurrently aggregates all candidate databases across both Classic Antigravity and Antigravity IDE (including `antigravity-ide` variants), resolving the issue where accounts logged in via the IDE were silently overlooked.
            -   **Independent Concurrent Antigravity CLI (agy) Credential Detection**: Introduced `read_antigravity_cli_credentials()` to bypass Keychain short-circuiting that previously masked CLI tokens. Independently scans Antigravity CLI credentials and backups (`~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token*`), strictly targeting Antigravity tokens for automated multi-source deduplication and onboarding.
        -   **[Independent Active Account Tracking & Contextual Multi-Client Badges for Classic, IDE & CLI (agy)] (Ref #3630, Ref #3686)**:
            -   **Independent Multi-Client Active Account Ground-Truth Detection**: Added `detect_active_clients_status` and `get_active_clients_status` Tauri commands to accurately determine the live logged-in account for each client environment independently — Antigravity Classic (Keychain / DB), Antigravity IDE (`state.vscdb`), and Antigravity CLI (`agy` credentials & Keyring). Resolves status collision and ambiguity caused by a single global `current_account_id`.
            -   **Persistent Multi-Target Switch Tracking**: Extended `AccountIndex` with `current_classic_account_id`, `current_ide_account_id`, and `current_agy_account_id` fields, persisting exact client-specific active assignments whenever an account is switched for a targeted client.
            -   **Contextual Multi-Client Badges & Row Highlighting**: Implemented distinct client badges (`[Classic]`, `[IDE]`, `[CLI]`) across both Card (`AccountCard`) and Table (`AccountTable`) views. Supports multi-badge combinations when an account is active in multiple clients, and highlights borders/rows with the active theme tint for superior readability across multi-client workflows.
            -   **Complete Multi-Language Localization**: Synchronized badges and explanatory hover tooltips across English, Simplified Chinese, and Traditional Chinese.

The backend inspects the active model from local configuration in real-time, pre-selecting it on the UI and providing immediate feedback upon synchronization.

Intelligently decomposes composite model names into base models and thinking effort levels (e.g. `gemini-3.8-flash-low` -> `--model gemini-3.8-flash --effort low`), preventing known-model resolution errors from silently downgrading to cloud backends.

            -   **Case-Insensitive Parsing for Rate-Limit Retry-After Duration**: Fixed an issue where `extract_retry_after_seconds` failed to parse cooldown seconds after the rate-limit message was updated to `"All accounts rate-limited. Cooldown wait Ns."`, due to hardcoded uppercase `"Wait "` matching. Added chained matching for both `"Wait "` and `"wait "`, ensuring 503 responses reliably emit standard `Retry-After: N` headers so downstream clients (such as Claude Code and OpenCode) back off accurately without blind retries or prolonged stalls. (PR #3687, Fixes #3506, Thanks to @EricZhou05)
        -   **[Complete Elimination of 0ms Optimistic Reset Storms, Physical Cooldown Backoff & Endpoint Health Decoupling] (PR #3685, Fixes #3506, Ref #3525, Ref #3634, Ref #3666, Ref #3680, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Eradicate 0ms Optimistic Reset Storms & Introduce Physical Async Cooldown**: Completely removed `clear_for_optimistic_reset()` from `token_manager`, stopping the gateway from blindly zero-delay replaying requests across all limited accounts and triggering cascading hard lockouts during concurrent 429 bursts. When the minimum account pool cooldown is `<= 5s`, executes real physical async sleep (`wait_sec * 1000 + 100ms`) to allow rate limits to naturally recover; when wait time is `> 5s`, explicitly returns a cooldown requirement error prompting standard client-side backoff. (PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Two-Round Adaptive Paced Rotation & Stepped Backoff Retries**: Relaxed Round 1 pool rotation delay from 50ms to 150ms to grant a micro-cooldown window; expanded the request-level 429 fail-fast threshold from 1 round to 2 rounds (`pool_size * 2`), introducing linear stepped backoff (1.5s ~ 3.5s) in Round 2 to accommodate large prompt rate limit recovery while preventing indefinite stalls. Verified in production to slash high-concurrency 429 error rates from 37.4% down to 0.0%. (PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Strictly Decoupled Background Quota Probing from Primary Inference Health**: Created a dedicated, independent `QUOTA_ENDPOINT_HEALTH` tracker (60-second observation window) in `modules/quota.rs` with probe timeouts extended to 15 seconds, completely preventing background periodic quota and `project_id` polling timeouts from degrading the primary inference gateway's endpoints. (PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Adaptive Candidate Endpoint Watchdog & Tightened HTTP/2 Idle Pool**: Watchdog fail-safe timeout now dynamically scales based on request body size (15s baseline, plus 1s per 256KB), preventing 11MB+ large prompts from premature failover while uploading; tightened HTTP/2 `pool_idle_timeout` to 15 seconds and capped `pool_max_idle_per_host` at 10 to eliminate reusing dead TCP connections silently terminated by Google GFE. (PR #3685, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Official Antigravity Native Identity Alignment & Tool Function Safe Sanitization] (Fixes #3684)**:
            -   **Official Antigravity Native Identity Alignment**: Addressed Google's recent heightened semantic feature rate limiting against external harnesses (e.g. Claude Code CLI, OpenCode, DSH, Pi, Codex), which triggered spurious 429 errors. Introduced the official native Antigravity identity statement (`OFFICIAL_ANTIGRAVITY_IDENTITY`) in `PromptSanitizer`, harmonizing external client preambles and broad attribution statements to mirror the official IDE identity, completely removing harness fingerprints and securing parity with official IDE quota and rate-limit treatment. (Fixes #3684)
            -   **Tool Function (Tools) Description Defensive Sanitization**: Prevented WAF blocks triggered by billing headers (e.g. `x-anthropic-billing-header:`) or SDK fingerprints injected into tool declarations (`tools[].functionDeclarations[].description`). Added header-track defensive filtering to tool descriptions in the inbound pipeline while 100% preserving function names (`name`) and parameter schemas (`parameters`), guaranteeing smooth, error-free autonomous agent tool execution. (Fixes #3684)
        -   **[Traffic Monitor Detail Redesign: Unified Single-Tab View, Structured Payload Inspection & Dual-Link HAR Export]**:
            -   **Unified Postman-Style Single-Tab Container**: Refactored the detail modal into "Request", "Forwarded", and "Response" primary tabs, eliminating split-screen compression and maximizing vertical inspection space alongside inline token pills.
            -   **Structured Payload & Raw Code Dual Views**: 1-click toggle to intuitively inspect system prompts, multi-turn message bubbles, tool declarations, thinking blocks, and function call receipts.
            -   **Key-Value Headers Table & Masking**: Modernized headers and URL query params into interactive 2-column tables with real-time search, hover copy, and `Bearer sk-***` masking.
            -   **Standard Dual-Link HAR 1.2 Export (All 3 Payloads Complete)**: Generates dual-link HAR entries (Client->Gateway and Gateway->Upstream), guaranteeing complete retention of inbound request, forwarded payload, and server response; supports 1-click cURL and JSON copying.
            -   **Full 12-Language i18n Support**: All new UI components, labels, and feedback messages are fully localized across all 12 supported system languages.

        -   **[/v1/models Dynamic Capabilities & Metadata Pass-through with Bare Model Inheritance] (Fixes #3677)**:
            -   **Full Model Capabilities Metadata Exposure (ModelListingMeta)**: Resolved an issue where downstream aggregation gateways (e.g. One API, New API) and clients could not discover model capabilities. Directly surfaces upstream capability attributes from in-memory account quota data across `/v1/models` and `/v1/models/:model` (OpenAI, Responses, and Claude endpoints), including `display_name`, `supports_images`, `supports_thinking`, `thinking_budget`, `recommended`, `max_tokens`, `max_output_tokens`, and `supported_mime_types`. (Fixes #3677)
            -   **Automated Capability Inheritance for Derived Bare Models**: When upstream accounts only report tiered variants (e.g. `gemini-3.7-flash-high`), the gateway automatically derives the bare model (`gemini-3.7-flash`) while inheriting all capability flags from the tier variant and sanitizing display name parentheticals, ensuring standard bare model names never lose image or thinking support. (Fixes #3677)
            -   **In-Memory Caching & Backward Compatibility**: Model metadata is cached in-memory during account loading and quota refreshes with zero recurring disk I/O; all capability fields are optional with null-skipping, maintaining 100% backward compatibility with legacy account configurations. (Fixes #3677)
        -   **[In-App Model Connectivity Probe, Account Pool Dispatch & Request Waterfall Integration] (Ref #3678)**:
            -   **Out-of-the-Box In-App Model Probing**: Introduced an instant connectivity probe in the "Supported Models & Integration" table. Users can verify model availability and latency with a single click without configuring third-party clients or curl scripts; supports batch testing ("⚡️ Probe All") with cancellation. (Ref #3678)
            -   **Dual Mode: Smart Pool Auto-Dispatch & Targeted Account Selection**: Defaults to "🤖 Auto Dispatch (Pool)" which filters out disabled accounts and prioritizes healthy accounts with sufficient quota; supports selecting a specific account via the header dropdown for granular diagnosis. (Ref #3678)
            -   **Integrated with SQLite Traffic Logs & Endpoint Waterfall**: Each probe request is saved as a standard `warmup` protocol entry in SQLite logs. Clicking status badges (e.g. `🟢 280ms`, `🟡 429 Limited`, `🔴 Retired`) opens the full request detail modal, displaying upstream endpoint attempt waterfalls (Prod / Daily / Sandbox latency), TTFT stage breakdowns, and complete headers/payloads. (Ref #3678)
            -   **Non-Intrusive IPC Architecture & Full 12-Language i18n**: Powered by clean Tauri IPC commands without bypassing CSP or CORS rules; delivers a refined table UI and synchronizes translations across all 12 supported languages. (Ref #3678)
        -   **[12s Upstream Watchdog, Account Status Sync Fix & Claude Code Support] (PR #3680, Fixes #3506, Ref #3525, Ref #3634, Ref #3666, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Candidate Endpoint 12s Streaming Watchdog Timeout**: Introduced a 12-second watchdog timeout (`tokio::time::timeout`) on candidate endpoint handshakes (`send().await`) when fallback endpoints are available. Prevents silent 56-second stalls on unresponsive upstream endpoints by promptly failing over to the next candidate; extended `EndpointHealthTracker` failure cooldown to 300 seconds (5 minutes) to avoid premature reconnection to degraded endpoints. (PR #3680, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Account Entity Authority & Index Drift Fix**: Established account entity files as the authoritative source of truth for enabled/disabled states, fixing issues where index drift in `accounts.json` could erroneously disable healthy accounts and automatically self-healing index flags upon reload. (PR #3680, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Claude Code 2.1.300+ Compatibility & 1M Context Alias Support**: Updated `canonicalize_claude_client_model_id` to strip square-bracket context window suffixes (e.g. `[1m]`, `[200k]`), seamlessly matching custom and built-in model mappings; extracted top-level `effort` parameter for pipeline thinking budget calculation and merged client `anthropic-beta` headers. (PR #3680, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Deeply Nested Tool Schema Recursion Cap Lift & Bare Array Fallback Fix] (Fixes #3682)**:
            -   **Recursion Depth Cap Decoupled & Raised (MAX_RECURSION_DEPTH = 64)**: Resolved an issue where deeply nested tool schemas (e.g. recursive `children` arrays in mindmap generators) exceeding depth 10 skipped fallback sanitization in `clean_json_schema_recursive`, forwarding bare arrays (without `items`) upstream and triggering `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT: missing field.`. Raised the recursion cap from 10 to 64, seamlessly supporting 30+ levels of tree nesting. (Fixes #3682)
            -   **Eliminate Erroneous Schema-Flatten Warnings**: Decoupled `$ref` expansion from structural cleaning limits and guarded recursion warnings to only fire when an unexpanded `$ref` is present, eliminating spurious `WARN [Schema-Flatten] Max recursion depth reached` log spam on pure nested schemas. (Fixes #3682)
            -   **Iterative Fail-Safe Compliance Fallback**: Implemented an iterative heap-based `apply_minimal_failsafe` normalizer that guarantees child arrays receive fallback `items: {"type": "string"}` and objects receive appropriate types even when hitting extreme recursion thresholds, avoiding call stack overflow while preventing non-compliant Protobuf payloads from reaching Gemini. (Fixes #3682)
        -   **[Official Hub Fingerprint Alignment, Remote Manifest Dynamic Detection & User Overrides] (Ref #3665)**:
            -   **Align with Official Hub 2.23.1 & CL 998881661**: Upgraded official upstream User-Agent headers from outdated 2.17.0 to the latest official Hub version `2.23.1` and Piper Monorepo changelist `cl=998881661` (`antigravity/hub/2.23.1 (aidev_client; os_type=darwin; arch=arm64; cl=998881661)`), preventing rejection or 429 anomalies caused by obsolete client identification or mistakenly embedding the gateway's own release version. (Ref #3665)
            -   **4-Tier Adaptive Resolution (Custom > Remote Manifest > Local Installation > Baseline Fallback)**: Added dynamic `hub_version` module supporting zero-restart fetching of the latest Hub version directly from the official Cloud Run updater manifest, automatically inspecting local Antigravity.app installation metadata, and gracefully falling back to built-in baseline constants if offline or uninstalled. (Ref #3665)
            -   **Visual Hub Fingerprint Card & Granular Customization**: Introduced an "Official Hub Request Fingerprint" card under Settings -> Advanced displaying real-time User-Agent strings and active resolution sources (Custom, Remote, Local, Baseline). Equipped with a "🔄 Dynamic Probe" button, one-click clipboard copy, and granular version/CL override inputs persisted in configuration. (Ref #3665)
        -   **[Bidirectional Tool Call/Response Self-Healing & Orphan unknown Fallback Protection] (Fixes #3679)**:
            -   **Pipeline-Grade Tool Name Topology Self-Healing**: Resolved an issue where client sliding window truncation, formatting drift, or missing adapter mappings resulted in undeclared `'unknown'` tool names in forwarded requests, triggering fatal upstream Gemini 400 Bad Request errors. Built a session-wide `id -> name` bidirectional topology index in the inbound pipeline that automatically heals missing or `'unknown'` tool response names from canonicalized tool call IDs and preceding turns. (Fixes #3679)
            -   **Smooth Fallback for Orphan Unknown Tool Responses**: When context is completely truncated and tool responses cannot be associated with any declared tool call, safely degrades the orphan response into a structured plain text part (`[Tool Output]: ...`), preserving critical tool execution results for the model while eliminating fatal upstream 400 rejection. (Fixes #3679)
            -   **OpenAI & Responses Adapter Reverse-Lookup Hardening**: Enhanced Chat Completions mapping to look back across multiple preceding turns (skipping intermediate thinking/text blocks) to locate matching tool call IDs, bridged normalized ID differences, and inferred tool names when only a single tool is declared; strengthened Responses API to extract nested `function.name` properties. (Fixes #3679)
        -   **[Dedicated User Token Usage Analytics & Cyber Cockpit User Rankings] (Fixes #3676)**:
            -   **Granular Visual Analytics for Individual Tokens (UserTokenStatsModal)**: Addressed the limitation where global Token Statistics focused solely on upstream Google accounts and models without key-level drilling. Users can now click the usage cell or the dedicated "📊 Stats" button in the "User Tokens" table to open a detailed usage analytics modal. (Fixes #3676)
            -   **Usage Trend Area Chart & Key KPI Cards**: Supports switching across 24h (hourly resolution), 7 Days (daily resolution), 30 Days, and All-time spans. Renders an interactive Recharts area chart distinguishing input vs. output tokens alongside 4 core KPI cards: Total Tokens, Total Requests (with success rate), Avg. Tokens/Request, and Bound Client IPs. (Fixes #3676)
            -   **Model Breakdown & Client IP Attribution**: Aggregates token consumption, percentage share, and request counts by model; surfaces active client IPs with invocation frequencies, recent activity timestamps, and User-Agent headers. (Fixes #3676)
            -   **Cyber Cockpit User Rankings & Instant Drill-Down**: Added a 3rd "Users" tab to the right-side ranking panel in the Cyber Cockpit View alongside "Accounts" and "Models". Provides a streamlined single-line tab switcher ("Accounts" | "Models" | "Users"), displays top active user token consumption with relative progress bars and percentages, and opens the detailed usage modal directly upon clicking any item. (Fixes #3676)
            -   **Optimized Backend Aggregations & Headless API Parity**: Leverages indexed `token_usage_logs` with dedicated `get_user_token_stats` and `get_user_tokens_ranking` Tauri commands alongside `/api/user-tokens/:id/stats` and `/api/user-tokens/ranking` admin endpoints, maintaining strict feature parity across desktop and headless environments. (Fixes #3676)
            -   **Layout Anti-Wrapping Hardening & Full 12-Language i18n**: Fixed layout wrapping issues for lengthy usernames, status badges, and segmented time pills; delivered complete localized strings across all 12 supported languages (zh, zh-TW, en, ja, ko, es, ru, ar, vi, tr, pt, my). (Fixes #3676)

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.5 (2026-10-15)**:
        -   **[Switch Quota & Model Discovery UA to Official Hub Identity with Dynamic Versioning] (Ref #3665, Thanks to @wd2es7)**:
            -   **Switch Upstream Discovery UA to Hub Subclient**: Fixed an issue where ULTRA accounts could not discover or route `gemini-4-argon` models. Following community insights from @wd2es7 and reverse engineering of `/Applications/Antigravity.app`, verified that Google gates Argon availability based on the client `subclient_type` in the User-Agent (`ide`/`vscode` filters out Argon yielding 35 models, whereas `hub`/`cli` returns all 38 models including Argon). Consolidated `fetch_project_id`, `fetch_quota_with_cache`, and `fetch_quota_summary` to official `antigravity/hub` UA. (Ref #3665, Thanks to @wd2es7)
            -   **Dynamic Versioning & OS Fingerprint**: Eliminated hardcoded version `2.17.0` in `constants::USER_AGENT`, replacing it with `build_official_upstream_user_agent()` which dynamically embeds the current app version (e.g. `4.9.8-beta.5`) and host architecture/platform (darwin/linux/windows and arm64/x64), ensuring upstream headers stay synchronized with releases. (Ref #3665)
            -   **Official Model Catalog Alignment**: Registered `gemini-4-argon` in offline fallback `official_models.json` aligned with upstream `minThinkingBudget: 32` metadata. (Ref #3665)
        -   **[Gemini 4 Argon Runtime Thinking Budget Safeguard & 400 Error Mitigation] (Ref #3665)**:
            -   **Runtime Minimum Thinking Budget Guard (≥ 1024)**: Upstream verification revealed that despite Google's metadata indicating `minThinkingBudget: 32`, sending any non-zero thinking budget $< 1024$ to `generateContent` triggers `400 Thinking level is unsupported: THINKING_LEVEL_MINIMAL`. Added an inbound pipeline clamp in `inbound.rs` and aligned `gemini-4-argon-low` in `model_specs.rs` to enforce a runtime floor of `1024`, completely preventing upstream 400 failures. (Ref #3665)

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.4 (2026-10-15)**:
        -   **[Real-Time Event Header Retention & Instant TTFT Rendering Fix]**:
            -   **Retain Response Headers in Event Broadcast**: Fixed an issue where `ProxyRequestLog::summary` stripped `response_headers` during real-time event broadcasting, preventing the frontend from extracting `X-Timing-Ttft-Ms` dynamically and causing first-token metrics to show as empty ("-") until the view was refreshed.
            -   **Lightweight Real-Time Delivery**: Kept heavy request and response bodies stripped while retaining lightweight response headers, ensuring zero IPC latency while rendering first-token timings instantly upon request completion.

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.3 (2026-10-15)**:
        -   **[Non-Streaming TTFT Header Hydration, Raw Upstream Error Transparency & Pure Native Timing UI]**:
            -   **Non-Streaming TTFT Measurement Coverage**: Fixed missing `X-Timing-Ttft-Ms` in Claude non-streaming responses (such as prompt compaction summaries) that caused empty ("-") first-token values in the log table; added dual-source TTFT extraction ensuring 100% TTFT availability across all streaming and non-streaming calls.
            -   **Transparent Raw Error Payload in Fallback Attempts**: In `UpstreamClient`, captured and sanitized raw upstream response bodies during retryable 5xx/timeout failures, directly displaying the official upstream error payload in the diagnostic timeline card.
            -   **Streamlined Native Timing Stage Labels**: Completely eliminated mixed English parentheses across all Chinese timing stage labels for pure native localization; nested fallback overhead duration directly beneath the TTFT stage to keep the multi-stage progress bar perfectly balanced.
            -   **Base64 Pass-Through for Attempt Traces**: Injected `X-Timing-Ttft-Attempts` as a Base64-encoded response header, eliminating async task context drops and allowing the frontend to reconstruct complete fallback timelines under any conditions.

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.2 (2026-10-14)**:
        -   **[Full fetchAvailableModels Dynamic Catalog Alignment, Removal of Static Hardcoded Models & Adaptive Support] (Ref #3665)**:
            -   **Native Authorized Model Catalog (available_models)**: Added `available_models: HashSet<String>` to `ProxyToken`, mirroring the raw model catalog delivered live by Google's `fetchAvailableModels`. Acts as the single source of truth for account capabilities, enabling fully adaptive model dispatching. (Ref #3665)
            -   **Abolish Static Gate & 3-Step Dynamic Matching**: Completely eliminated the legacy `model_quotas.contains_key(normalized)` veto gate. Replaced it with a 3-step dynamic matching pipeline (Step A exact matching on raw ID, Step B tier suffix stripping via `strip_tier_suffix` to match base model variants, Step C fallback to generic wildcard patterns only when all accounts are in cold start), completely eliminating false-positive 503 rejection on unindexed models. (Ref #3665)
            -   **Prune Redundant CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI Self-Mappings**: Purged all redundant X -> X self-mappings for native `gemini-*` and `claude-*` models from `CLAUDE_TO_GEMINI`, delegating transparent routing to dynamic pass-through and `DYNAMIC_MODEL_FORWARDING_RULES`; kept only true cross-vendor aliases (e.g., `gpt-*`) and deprecated version transitions. (Ref #3665)
            -   **Completely Remove Specific Codename (argon) Hardcodings**: Refactored `is_gemini_v4_or_above` in `model_specs.rs` into a pure numeric/semantic version parser (`gemini-[4-9]`, `>= 4.0`), removing all hardcoded `"argon"` string checks. Upgraded `get_thinking_budget` and `get_max_output_tokens` to generic tier deductions and base-name dynamic fallbacks, enabling zero-code-change support for future frontier models. (Ref #3665)
        -   **[Imminent Quota Floor to Eliminate False 429s & Stream Truncation Golden Triplet Contract] (PR #3666, Fixes #429, Fixes #458, Fixes #3635, Fixes #3661, Ref #3626, Ref #3639, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Imminent Quota Floor (IMMINENT_QUOTA_FLOOR)**: Enforced a quota floor (`<= 10%`) in EDF and P2C scheduling algorithms where critically depleted accounts are stripped of imminent reset deadline boosting and strictly fall back to sorting by absolute quota. Eradicates false-positive `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` errors caused by 1%~2% accounts placed at head-of-line that instantly trigger 429 and exhaust circuit-breaker retry budgets. (PR #3666, Fixes #3661, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Atomic Proxy Switch File and Index Synchronization**: Refactored `toggle_proxy_status` to atomically sync individual account credentials and `accounts.json` under an `ACCOUNT_INDEX_LOCK` mutex, preventing state drift. (PR #3666, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Golden Triplet Termination Contract & Token Preservation**: Added content generation detection (`has_content()`) in `stream_lifecycle.rs`. When deep reasoning or long-running streams encounter network disconnection or idle timeouts after generating content, the gateway gracefully emits `content_block_stop`, `message_delta(stop_reason: end_turn)`, and `message_stop` golden triplet sequence to satisfy client completion state machines (such as Claude Code), activating client-side `keepPartial` preservation and eradicating token wastage and infinite re-thinking loops. (PR #3666, Fixes #429, Fixes #458, Fixes #3635, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.1 (2026-10-14)**:
        -   **[Refactored Column Resize Logic, Fixed Dashboard Dark Mode Card & Hardened Endpoint Tracing]**:
            -   **Linear Column Resizing & Overflow Isolation**: Completely eliminated the unexpected elastic column expansion side effect in `ProxyMonitor`, switching to deterministic 1:1 drag-to-resize; enforced `table-layout: fixed` with strict `overflow: hidden` across all cells, completely eliminating token usage text overlapping with service accounts.
            -   **Fixed Inverted Dashboard Health Card Theme**: Corrected the first dashboard card ("Account Base Assets") which incorrectly rendered bright white in dark mode and dark in light mode, restoring full aesthetic consistency.
            -   **Cross-Task Endpoint Trace Persistence & Unified Panel**: Fixed context loss in monitor middleware for async streaming tasks, ensuring attempts data persists cleanly; upgraded the timing diagnostics card to always render endpoint breakdown panels across both direct hits and fallbacks.
            -   **Eliminated Release Pipeline Collisions**: Removed tag triggers and added release guards to `build-windows.yml` to prevent unsigned binaries from overwriting official signed assets, permanently resolving "The signature verification failed" update errors.

    *   **v4.9.8-beta.0 (2026-10-14)**:
        -   **[Pure Point-to-Point TTFT Latency Decoupling & Dynamic Multi-Endpoint Fallback Trace]**:
            -   **Pure Point-to-Point TTFT Measurement**: Decoupled upstream timeout and retry attempts from first-token metrics. Individual timers are now recorded per endpoint in `UpstreamClient`, measuring pure TTFT strictly from the moment the winning endpoint dispatches until the first valid SSE chunk (thinking/tool/content) arrives.
            -   **Endpoint Fallback Breakdown & Switch Latency Tracking**: Added granular attempt tracking for each fallback hop (endpoint alias, URL, HTTP status code, attempt duration) along with system-level switch dispatch latency (`switch_delay_s`), consolidating prior failure times into `ttft_overhead_s`.
            -   **Interactive Fallback Timeline in ProxyMonitor**: In the timing diagnostics view, streamlined normal requests to clean single-stage views while dynamically expanding a timeline for degraded requests (displaying each attempt, switch latency, error status, and first-token hit), with multi-stage stacked bar progress and enhanced timing copying.

        -   **[Hardened Account-Switch Persistence, Instant Quota Depletion Marking & Safe Optimistic Reset] (PR #3664, Fixes #3639, Fixes #3637, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **Account Switch Downgrade Fallback Hardening (Fixes #3637)**: In `integration.rs`, ensured `apply_account_credentials` is called again after fully terminating the main process during cold restart or timeout fallbacks, eliminating race conditions where external client exit hooks could overwrite credentials with stale tokens. (PR #3664, Fixes #3637, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Preserve Exact CLI Argument Case**: Preserved original argument case snapshots in `process.rs`, limiting lowercase transformations strictly to internal helper process matching. Completely eliminates corrupted workspace paths and empty workspace errors on case-sensitive Linux and macOS file systems. (PR #3664, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **OpenAI 429 Atomic Fast Lockout**: Unified atomic `mark_rate_limited_fast` calls in `handle_chat_completions` and `handle_completions` to instantly lock in-memory rate limits within 0ms, eliminating redundant candidate re-selections during attempt 1 retries while background quota network checks are pending. (PR #3664, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Reactive In-Memory Quota Depletion Marking (Fixes #3639)**: Introduced `mark_model_quota_depleted` to reactively update `token.model_quotas` in-memory cache to 0% immediately upon receiving upstream `QuotaExhausted` errors, ensuring sticky sessions and preferred accounts auto-migrate seamlessly on subsequent turns. (PR #3664, Fixes #3639, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Optimistic Reset Quota Safeguard & Ceiling Wait Times**: Optimized `clear_for_optimistic_reset` to strictly retain all active `QuotaExhausted` entries and prioritize healthy candidates with positive quota; updated `rate_limit.rs` to compute remaining wait durations with ceiling rounding, eradicating premature wakeups and accidental routing to 0% accounts. (PR #3664, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
        -   **[Concurrency-Aware Traffic Spillover Scheduling & Max Spillover Cap] (PR #3662, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:
            -   **In-Flight Concurrency Tracking (InFlightGuard)**: Implemented an RAII guard tracking active requests per account atomically across streaming and non-streaming HTTP responses in Claude, Gemini, and OpenAI proxy handlers. (PR #3662, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Concurrency-Aware Traffic Spillover**: Enhanced `select_with_p2c` so that when primary accounts are saturated with active in-flight requests (`in_flight > 0`), subsequent concurrent requests smoothly spill over to idle/lower-load accounts to eliminate head-of-line blocking in multi-session and parallel agent workloads, while proactively bypassing 0% quota accounts to prevent 429 errors. (PR #3662, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Configurable Spillover Account Cap (max_spillover_accounts)**: Added support for capping the maximum number of distinct accounts concurrently engaged (0 for unlimited; N > 0 caps active accounts to N), with interactive slider controls and full translations in the Proxy Scheduling Settings UI. (PR #3662, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
        -   **[High-Resolution Minute-Level Token Analytics & Granular Trends] (PR #3663, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:
            -   **Backend Minute Aggregation Endpoints**: Introduced `get_minute_stats`, `get_model_trend_minute`, and `get_account_trend_minute` in `token_stats.rs`, aggregating usage by minute timestamp (`%Y-%m-%d %H:%M`) across 1h and 24h spans via Tauri IPC commands. (PR #3663, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)

            -   **Automated Deprecated Model Pruning**: Upgraded `useProxyModels` to dynamically inspect account `model_forwarding_rules` (from Google `deprecatedModelIds`), automatically pruning deprecated and redirected model aliases from client UI.
            -   **Active Family Baseline Convergence**: Automatically pruned obsolete transitional sub-versions (3.5, 3.6, 3.7) when a higher active minor version like `gemini-3.8-flash` is present in the account pool, keeping supported model listings sleek; added forward-looking high-contrast icons and groups for Gemini 4 Argon.
            -   **Capability-First Dynamic Scheduling for Gemini 4 Argon & Claude 5.x**: Generalized dynamic capability detection in `model_specs.rs` and `token_manager.rs` to strictly route requests only to accounts reporting active model capabilities in `model_quotas` / `model_limits`, eliminating accidental routing of canary models to ungranted accounts.

Tightened service stop bypass logic to completely eliminate quota draining, unauthorized upstream forwarding, and false account bans. (Ref #3659, PR #3660)

Chat streaming, project resolution (`loadCodeAssist`), and quota fetching (`fetchAvailableModels`, `retrieveUserQuotaSummary`) now seamlessly share failure cooldowns.             -   **Strict Whitelist Filtering for Safety Categories (Fixes #3195)**: Enforced strict whitelist filtering on `safetySettings` in the inbound pipeline (`inbound.rs`), retaining only the 5 valid categories officially recognized by Google Gemini (`HARASSMENT`, `HATE_SPEECH`, `SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT`, `DANGEROUS_CONTENT`, `CIVIC_INTEGRITY`). Completely eliminates `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT (element predicate failed)` errors caused by Codex `/v1/responses` passing deprecated or custom categories. (PR #3652, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Intelligent Shell Tool Name Alignment (Fixes #3224)**: Enhanced `resolve_shell_tool_name` in `response.rs` with client declaration awareness. When clients only declare `bash` while the model invokes `local_shell_call` or `shell_command`, automatically maps the call to `bash`, preventing client errors such as `Model tried to call unavailable tool 'local_shell_call'`. (PR #3652, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Pro Model Fallback Ordering Optimization & Output Token Floor (Fixes #3215)**: Optimized candidate ordering for `pro_family` in `token_manager.rs` to prioritize full-spec models (`gemini-3.1-pro-high` and `gemini-3.1-pro-preview`) over legacy `gemini-pro-agent` channels; enforced an 8,192 token floor in `get_max_output_tokens` within `model_specs.rs` to prevent long generations from being truncated by restrictive 2k agent limits. (PR #3652, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
        -   **[Account Card and List Weekly View Stacked Layout Polishing] (PR #3655, Ref #3189, Thanks to @ilyassbourass)**:
            -   **Stacked Presentation of Weekly & 5H Rolling Quotas**: Streamlined display behavior in `AccountCard.tsx` and `AccountTable.tsx` when switching to the 7-day Weekly view. Instead of replacing per-model 5H quotas entirely with generic weekly buckets, both are now stacked: per-model 5H quotas remain on top, while 7-day weekly buckets are placed underneath separated by a soft blue divider with tinted borders, providing instant visibility into both short-term availability and weekly budget caps. (PR #3655, Thanks to @ilyassbourass)
        -   **[Upstream Endpoint Dynamic Health Tracking & Transport Timeout Convergence] (PR #3657, Fixes #3635, Ref #3525, Ref #3593, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Dynamic Endpoint Health Tracker (EndpointHealthTracker)**: Introduced an in-memory health tracking mechanism backed by `DashMap` and a 60-second cooldown TTL in `upstream/client.rs`. Dynamically reorders candidate endpoints for streaming (`call_v1_internal_with_headers`) and auxiliary (`call_v1_internal_auxiliary`) requests: healthy endpoints are tried first, while endpoints currently in cooldown are deprioritized to the tail for probing, self-healing instantly upon a successful response. Completely eradicates stateless 40s cascading timeout loops on dead endpoints for subsequent in-session requests, stabilizing TTFT back to 1~2s native performance. (PR #3657, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Transport Connect and Idle Timeout Convergence**: Tightened `connect_timeout` from 20s to 5s to rapidly flag unreachable endpoints during initial probing, shrinking worst-case cascading failover delay from 40s to under 10s; and reduced `pool_idle_timeout` from 90s to 45s to match Google Front End (GFE) silent TCP termination windows, eliminating `error reading a body from connection` caused by reusing dead sockets. (PR #3657, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Pass-Through and Visualize Physical Upstream Model Versions] (PR #3658, Resolves #3654, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **X-Upstream-Model Response Header Normalization**: Injected the standard `X-Upstream-Model` header across OpenAI, Claude, Gemini, and Warmup protocol responses to provide full downstream transparency into the exact physical model deployed upstream. (PR #3658, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Physical Version Resolution & Full Observability**: The monitoring middleware now captures raw physical `modelVersion` signatures from SSE candidate streams and non-streaming responses, persisting them automatically into the `request_logs` database; fully surfaced in `ProxyMonitor` (rendered as `Requested => Mapped (⚡ Physical Upstream)`) and the `MiniView` floating bar. (PR #3658, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)

    *   **v4.9.7-beta.8 (2026-10-11)**:
        -   **[Token Stats Overview & Deep Insights Dashboard, Full-Stack Thinking (Reasoning) Stats & Unified Dark Theme]**:
            -   **Full-Stack Thinking / Reasoning Token Analytics**:
                -   **Database & Proxy Integration**: Seamlessly migrated proxy logs (`proxy_db`) and aggregate tables (`token_stats`) to track `reasoning_tokens`. Extracted model thinking token consumption automatically from streaming SSE chunks and non-streaming responses across all protocols.
                -   **Multi-Dimensional Aggregations**: Extended hourly, daily, weekly, all-time, account-level, and model-level statistical endpoints to return aggregated reasoning tokens and average reasoning intensity.
                -   **Overview Dashboard Integration**: Prominently featured reasoning tokens with a dedicated purple badge and output proportion (`Reasoning: XX (YY%)`) in the main usage overview card, along with average reasoning throughput per request in the J23 efficiency card header.
            -   **"Overview" Dashboard View Integration & Polishing**: Introduced the new Overview dashboard featuring a 360° radial ray gauge, core model breakdown with tooltip inspection, 24-hour activity bar pulse, J23 efficiency gauge, and account leaderboard with single-model highlight focus.
            -   **Dark Theme Background & Component Elevation Unification**: Eradicated fragmented hardcoded dark backgrounds (`dark:bg-[#0a0a0c]`, `dark:bg-[#121316]`, `dark:bg-[#1c1c1e]`, etc.), aligning entirely with global design tokens (card containers `dark:bg-base-100`, pills & segmented controls `dark:bg-base-200`, borders `dark:border-base-200/300`), eliminating seams against sticky navigation bars and harmonizing Classic vs new dashboard visual tones.
            -   **Full 12-Language Localization for Token Stats**: Fully synchronized `token_stats` i18n keys across all 12 supported locales (English, Spanish, Russian, Arabic, Japanese, Korean, Portuguese, Turkish, Vietnamese, Burmese, Simplified & Traditional Chinese), decoupling hardcoded week formatting.
            -   **Chart Tooltips & Custom Pricing Modal Dark Tuning**: Optimized dark mode backgrounds, borders, and input controls for chart tooltip overlays and the custom pricing modal (`PricingModal`), ensuring seamless tactile feedback across both light and dark modes.
        -   **[Intelligent Multi-Tier Scheduling: Weekly Quota EDF, Zero-Quota Session Auto-Failover, Cross-Model Isolation & Claude Compaction Hardening] (PR #3640, PR #3641, PR #3642, PR #3644, PR #3645, PR #3647, PR #3650, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9, @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Weekly Quota Earliest Deadline First Scheduling (EDF / Use-It-Or-Lose-It)**: Accurately extracted Google 7d/weekly bucket quota reset timestamps, introducing Earliest Deadline First rules (Priority 0.5) to multi-account rotation and P2C random selection. Prioritizes accounts approaching their reset window (weekly reset <= 24h or 5h bucket <= 2h with quota > 0%) to consume expiring capacity before scheduled resets, completely preventing usable allowances from going to waste under rigid high-quota-first policies. (PR #3645, Fixes #549, Ref #900, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Continuous Session & Preferred Account Zero-Quota Auto-Failover**: Added proactive quota awareness to Sticky Session and Preferred Account selection. When a bound account hits 0% quota for the requested model while healthy accounts remain available, atomically unbinds and re-routes requests before atomically rebinding the session upon success, preventing hard socket breaks and client interruptions during continuous AI Agent coding. (PR #3642, Fixes #3639, Ref #3509, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Cross-Model Quota Lockout Isolation**: Completely refactored `get_quota_reset_time` and `fetch_and_lock_with_realtime_quota` to strictly match normalized model IDs and only apply long-horizon reset lockouts when the target model's quota is truly exhausted (`percentage <= 0`). Prevents transient 429s on healthy models from inheriting days-long lockout timestamps from unrelated exhausted models (such as Claude Opus), eliminating false 503 Service Unavailable errors and pool starvation. (PR #3640, Fixes #3627, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Imagen 3 Micro-gap In-Place GraceRetry & Transient Cooldown Rescue**: Introduced micro-gap in-place recovery (`delay <= 2500ms`) during the first multi-account rotation round for Google image generation models, eradicating rapid 50ms cascading sweeps that exhausted whole pools within 300ms. Also introduced queue-level cooldown wait rescue in `get_image_token` within remaining timeout budgets, dramatically boosting image generation success rates. (PR #3641, Fixes #3329, Fixes #3409, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Claude Cowork Deep Compact State Machine & Continuation Tag Hardening**: Adjusted the sliding immunity lease window (`COMPACTION_IMMUNITY_WINDOW_SECS`) to 90 seconds to stably accommodate long reasoning models and complex multi-step MCP toolchains, completely resolving Double-400 false alarm loops. Expanded continuation scanning depth to the first 5 turns to pierce through multiple preceding `<system-reminder>` and environment detail blocks, while recognizing `/compact.` and `/compact <args>` command variants. (PR #3644, PR #3647, Ref #3576, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9, @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Claude Code Session Usage Scaling Bypass & Native 95% Auto-Compaction Self-Healing**: Unified `is_code_mode` detection logic to precisely recognize Claude Code CLI and Desktop Code Tab; forcibly bypassed usage scaling for Code mode requests when `enable_usage_scaling` is enabled to reflect raw physical token usage. Completely eradicates false low-usage signals that pacified client auto-compaction watchdogs, preventing sessions from failing to trigger `/compact` and abruptly disconnecting upon exceeding context limits. (PR #3650, Fixes #867, Fixes #1066, Fixes #1079, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Gemini Thinking Tier Alias Normalization & Inert Padding Degrade] (PR #3646, PR #3649, Fixes #3629, Ref #2208, Thanks to @ThermalEng, @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **Normalize Pro Thinking Tier Aliases to Upstream Physical ID**: Reused the unified model compatibility mapping to ensure bare `gemini-3.1-pro` routed to `high`, `medium` fallback, or default tier aliases, as well as explicit `gemini-3.1-pro-high`, are all normalized to the real upstream physical model ID `gemini-pro-agent`, completely resolving `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT` errors when querying `gemini-3.1-pro-high` upstream. (PR #3646, Ref #2208, Thanks to @ThermalEng)
            -   **Degrade Gemini Fallback User Padding to Inert Punctuation**: Downgraded fallback placeholder text (`TRANSIT_DEFENSE_FALLBACK_TEXT`) in `ensure_gemini_payload_ends_with_user` from natural language `"ok go on"` to inert punctuation `"."` for empty/substanceless user turns. Satisfies Gemini protocol non-empty `parts` constraints while completely preventing AI Coding Agents (such as Cline, Claude Code, OpenCode) from misinterpreting synthetic text as explicit user approval, permanently securing human-in-the-loop gates and eliminating loop cycles. (PR #3649, Fixes #3629, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Zero Thinking Budget Inbound Hard Cutoff & Soft Target Documentation**: When zero thinking budget is explicitly configured or derived via adaptive fast-response compression, strictly inject both `includeThoughts: false` and `thinkingBudget: 0`, achieving 100% parity with Google's native IDE protocol to completely extinguish upstream reasoning chains; additionally clarified in the settings UI across all 12 languages that upstream Gemini treats positive thinkingBudget as a heuristic soft target that can dynamically expand under complex prompts. (Ref #3648)
        -   **[HTTP API Field Restoration, Quiet Startup, Missing storage.json Self-Healing & Admin Hot-Sync] (PR #3651, Fixes #3631, Fixes #3630, Fixes #1411, Fixes #3270, Fixes #3275, Fixes #3280, Fixes #3260, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)**:
            -   **Full HTTP / Headless API Metadata Serialization**: Fully supplemented `validation_url`, `validation_blocked*`, `priority`, `disabled*`, `proxy_disabled*`, and `protected_models` metadata fields across `AccountResponse` in `modules/http_api.rs`, eliminating dead code in external panels and headless consumers to ensure unblock URLs and account statuses are transparently exposed. (PR #3651, Fixes #3631, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Validation Blocked State Complete Sanitization**: Concurrently cleared `validation_blocked_until`, `validation_url`, `validation_blocked_reason`, and reset `quota.is_forbidden = false` within `clear_validation_blocked` in `modules/account.rs`, relaxing cleanup guard conditions to eradicate circular lockout states caused by residual flags after manual unblocking. (PR #3651, Fixes #3630, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Comprehensive Quiet Startup Arguments Support**: Expanded `apply_login_launch_policy` CLI parameter checks to detect `--silent`, `--hidden`, `-s`, and `/minimized` in addition to `--minimized`, enabling seamless background tray launch from Windows Task Scheduler, batch scripts, and desktop shortcuts. (PR #3651, Fixes #1411, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Self-Healing Missing storage.json on Clean Install Profile Switching**: Enhanced `get_storage_path` and `write_profile` in `modules/device.rs` to automatically create parent directories and initialize fresh device profile configurations when IDE directories exist without a pre-generated `storage.json`, eliminating `storage_json_not_found` errors on initial switch. (PR #3651, Fixes #3270, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)

(PR #3651, Fixes #3275, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)

(PR #3651, Fixes #3280, Fixes #3285, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)
            -   **Extended Linux RPM Installation Path Detection**: Added standard `/usr/lib64` and `/usr/lib` paths to Linux IDE executable detection in `modules/process.rs` for Fedora, openSUSE, and RHEL distributions. (PR #3651, Fixes #3260, Thanks to @modded-soldier-9)

        -   **[Align Claude Code 2.1.293 Termination State Machine & Eliminate Hangs and Truncation] (PR #3634, Ref #3633, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Unbuffered Streaming & Official SSE Keep-Alive**: Removed frontend blocking Peek for streaming requests to initiate immediate SSE streams upon HTTP 200, dispatching official standard SSE ping frames (`event: ping`) during idle windows to eradicate downstream 300s/5-minute watchdog freezes. (PR #3634, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Preserve Tool Result Loops via Context Folding**: In the Gemini target role normalization phase, seamlessly fold system injected text like `<system-reminder>` into prior `functionResponse` outputs to avoid dangling trailing `user` turns that break Gemini tool execution state machines. (PR #3634, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Termination State Awareness & Immediate Disconnect**: Implemented `is_terminal()` to sense `message_stop`, closing downstream connections within milliseconds after the termination frame to block out-of-order upstream frame poisoning; completely removed synthetic empty completions and pseudo-clean cutoffs, faithfully propagating errors to activate client native retries. (PR #3634, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Error Code Normalization & Retry-After Clamping**: Normalized status 529 to 429 and 403 to 503 to prevent false 10~30 minute overload locks or forced logouts; safely clamped `Retry-After` within `[1, 12]` seconds to eradicate extreme delay freezes. (PR #3634, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Standalone Exclusive Support for Language Server Hot-Switch without Window Restarts] (Fixes #3637, Thanks to @rassvetpublic-spec)**:
            -   **Standalone Exclusive Hot-Switch Gate**: Targeted language server hot-switching specifically to Antigravity Standalone (>= 2.0.0) clients (write credentials → kill `language_server` subprocess → re-assert authoritative credentials). Leveraged the parent host supervisor to respawn the child and reload new credentials without closing the main window, eliminating window flashing and preserving unsaved buffers and terminal sessions. (Fixes #3637, Thanks to @rassvetpublic-spec)
            -   **IDE & Error Fallback Safety Net (Risky Path Replacement)**: IDE variants retain full restart due to hard dependencies on Extension Host in-memory contexts and SQLite single-writer locks; additionally, if Standalone fails to find child processes or times out during respawn, smoothly falls back to full restart. (Fixes #3637)
        -   **[Token Stats Dashboard Persistence, Granular Ranges, Cost Metrics & Heatmap UX] (Ref PR #3638, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)**:
            -   **Dashboard Preferences Persistence**: Automatically saves and restores user dashboard preferences (dashboard mode, time granularity, chart type, dimension, and metric) via `localStorage` across page reloads. (Ref PR #3638, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Granular Time Ranges**: Added ISO calendar weekly aggregation range (past 12 weeks labeled with `Wxx`), alongside standardized Hourly (24 hours), Daily (14 days), and All (all-time history). (Ref PR #3638, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
            -   **Cost Metrics & Heatmap Visual Tuning**: Added dynamic toggling between Token throughput and estimated cost ($ USD) on trend charts, equipped overview cards with cost metrics, and enlarged heatmap cells to 16px with 5px gap for improved readability. (Ref PR #3638, Thanks to @zepeng-jin)
        -   **[Self-Contained Page Entrance Transitions & Card Micro-Interactions] (PR #3636, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Self-Contained Smooth Page Entrances**: Decoupled from global Router exit transitions by applying self-contained entry animations across 7 core pages (Dashboard, Accounts, Token Stats, API Proxy, Settings, Security, and Monitor), eradicating route switching jitter, flicker, and blank screens. (PR #3636, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Decoupled Scroll Viewport & GPU Acceleration**: Retained native fluid scroll calculation while equipping inner animated containers with hardware-accelerated synthesis to prevent Layout Thrashing. (PR #3636, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Card & Button Tactile Feedback**: Unified subtle hover elevation on cards and tactile tap scale on buttons, disabling SVG tween recalculations in charts during page mount to avoid main thread contention. (PR #3636, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)

    *   **v4.9.7-beta.5 (2026-10-09)**:
        -   **[Align Upstream Rules: Deprecate Model Terminal Turn Padding & Protect AI Coding Agent State Machines] (Fixes #3629)**:
            -   **Deprecate Trailing User Padding for Payloads Ending with Model Turn**: Aligned with Google upstream's latest protocol changes which allow requests ending with a `model` / `assistant` turn. Completely deprecated injecting synthetic user turns (`"ok go on"`) into trailing model turns. This eliminates issues where interactive AI Coding Agents (such as Cline, OpenCode, Pi Agent, Claude Code, etc.) waiting on human-in-the-loop confirmation gates (destructive Git operations, prompt questions) were bypassed by synthetic user approval, and prevents conversation history pollution and infinite loops caused by repeated `"ok go on"` injections after tool execution. (Fixes #3629)
        -   **[Precisely Remove Account Rotation Timeout Fuses & Outer Redundant Timeouts; Break Sticky Deadlock on First Chunk Retry] (PR #3628, Fixes #3626, Ref PR #3622, PR #3625, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Remove Account Rotation Cumulative Timeout Fuses**: Removed wall-clock cumulative 120s timeout fuses based on `request_start` in `claude.rs` and `openai.rs` retry loops, preventing false HTTP 504 Gateway Timeout errors during normal multi-account failover. (Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Remove Redundant Outer 120s Claude Stream Timeout**: Removed the hardcoded outer 120s `tokio::time::timeout` wrapper in `claude.rs`, fully delegating stream lifecycle to the inner staged gradient timeout pipeline and ensuring large code blocks and deep reasoning complete uninterrupted. (Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Break Sticky Session Deadlock on First-Chunk Timeout Retry**: Explicitly set `force_rotate = true` when first-chunk Peek times out, breaking sticky affinity deadlocks on stalled accounts. (Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
        -   **[Eliminate Fake Truncation on Stream Interruption & Extend Sliding Timeout to 120s to Guarantee Native Client Retries] (PR #3625, Ref #3621, PR #3622, Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)**:
            -   **Eradicate Fake Truncation & Synthetic End Frames**: Completely removed `emit_interruption_truncation` and synthetic `message_stop` / `[DONE]` frames, transparently passing through connection resets and timeouts so downstream clients (such as Claude Code CLI) immediately trigger official exponential backoff retries without corrupting conversation history with fake truncation text. (Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Extend Steady-State Streaming Sliding Timeout to 120s**: Extended the sliding window idle timeout from 45s to 120s to provide ample buffer for complex upstream code block pre-computation, eliminating false cutoff. (Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Frontend Timeout Alignment to 120s**: Aligned frontend timeout settings to 120s and removed OpenAI localized hardcoded timeout values. (Ref #3621, PR #3622, PR #3625)

(Thanks to @cubelikeplayDaniel)
            -   **Eradicate Heartbeat Reset Dead-Wait & Overload Retry Loops**: Removed Peek heartbeat reset dead-waits and lack of global request budgets causing rotation deadlocks, resolving infinite retry loops triggered by `overloaded_error` that caused 20+ minute freezes.
